In `qal/crates/qal-run/src/host.rs`, replace:

```rust
fn hidden(rel: &Path) -> bool {
    rel.components().any(|c| c.as_os_str().to_string_lossy().starts_with('.'))
}

#[cfg(unix)]
fn same_file(a: &std::fs::Metadata, b: &std::fs::Metadata) -> bool {
    use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
    a.dev() == b.dev() && a.ino() == b.ino()
}

#[cfg(not(unix))]
fn same_file(_: &std::fs::Metadata, _: &std::fs::Metadata) -> bool {
    true
}

/// `O_NOFOLLOW` where its value is known; `fs` is refused elsewhere (and on non-Unix targets,
/// where `same_file` cannot verify the opened file) instead of silently opening less safely.
const O_NOFOLLOW: Option<i32> = if cfg!(all(
    unix,
    any(target_os = "macos", target_os = "ios", target_os = "freebsd", target_os = "netbsd", target_os = "openbsd", target_os = "dragonfly")
)) {
    Some(0x0100)
} else if cfg!(all(
    any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "android"),
    any(target_arch = "aarch64", target_arch = "arm", target_arch = "powerpc", target_arch = "powerpc64", target_arch = "m68k")
)) {
    Some(0o100000)
} else if cfg!(all(
    any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "android"),
    any(
        target_arch = "x86",
        target_arch = "x86_64",
        target_arch = "riscv32",
        target_arch = "riscv64",
        target_arch = "loongarch64",
        target_arch = "s390x",
        target_arch = "mips",
        target_arch = "mips64",
        target_arch = "sparc",
        target_arch = "sparc64"
    )
)) {
    Some(0o400000)
} else {
    None
};

/// `O_NONBLOCK` where its value is known: opening a FIFO must not wait for a writer or reader.
const O_NONBLOCK: Option<i32> = if cfg!(all(
    unix,
    any(target_os = "macos", target_os = "ios", target_os = "freebsd", target_os = "netbsd", target_os = "openbsd", target_os = "dragonfly")
)) {
    Some(0x0004)
} else if cfg!(all(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "android"), any(target_arch = "mips", target_arch = "mips64"))) {
    Some(0o200)
} else if cfg!(all(any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "android"), any(target_arch = "sparc", target_arch = "sparc64"))) {
    Some(0x4000)
} else if cfg!(all(
    any(target_os = "linux", target_os = "android"),
    any(
        target_arch = "aarch64",
        target_arch = "arm",
        target_arch = "powerpc",
        target_arch = "powerpc64",
        target_arch = "m68k",
        target_arch = "x86",
        target_arch = "x86_64",
        target_arch = "riscv32",
        target_arch = "riscv64",
        target_arch = "loongarch64",
        target_arch = "s390x"
    )
)) {
    Some(0o4000)
} else {
    None
};

fn require_nofollow(flag: Option<i32>) -> Result<i32, String> {
    flag.ok_or_else(|| "the `fs` effect is not supported on this platform: files cannot be opened without following symbolic links".into())
}

/// The flags every `fs` open uses (`O_NOFOLLOW | O_NONBLOCK`), or why `fs` is unavailable here.
fn require_open_flags() -> Result<i32, String> {
    let nofollow = require_nofollow(O_NOFOLLOW)?;
    let nonblock = O_NONBLOCK.ok_or("the `fs` effect is not supported on this platform: files cannot be opened without blocking")?;
    Ok(nofollow | nonblock)
}

/// Opens for reading, or for writing (created if missing, not truncated: the caller truncates once
/// verified), never following a final symlink and never blocking (FIFOs, devices).
///
/// Known limitation: only the final path component is opened without following links, so a
/// co-located process that swaps a directory of the path for a symlink between `check_path` and
/// this call can make a write create an EMPTY file outside the granted directories (`verify_opened`
/// then refuses to write to it). The proper fix, opening with `openat` relative to a verified
/// parent directory descriptor, is planned for Phase 3.
fn open_nofollow(path: &Path, write: bool) -> std::io::Result<File> {
    let flag = require_open_flags().map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    let mut o = std::fs::OpenOptions::new();
    if write {
        o.write(true).create(true);
    } else {
        o.read(true);
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        o.custom_flags(flag);
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
```

```rust
/// Per-execution budgets besides fuel, time and memory (Phase 3 spec §2.5, §5.4).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Limits {
    /// Bytes `write_file` may write in total.
    pub fs_write_bytes: usize,
    /// Number of `write_file` calls.
    pub fs_writes: usize,
}

impl Default for Limits {
    fn default() -> Limits {
        Limits { fs_write_bytes: 64 << 20, fs_writes: 256 }
    }
}

/// Which budget an execution used up: reported as `budget_exceeded` (E0522).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct BudgetHit {
    /// `fs_bytes` or `fs_writes`.
    pub kind: &'static str,
    pub budget: usize,
    pub used: usize,
}
```

```rust
impl HostState {
    /// A fresh state for one execution.
    pub fn new(perms: Permissions, stdin: Box<dyn BufRead + Send>, limit: usize, budgets: Limits) -> HostState {
        HostState {
            failure: None,
            stdout: Vec::new(),
            stdin,
            perms,
            rng: std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_nanos() as u64).unwrap_or(1),

            limit,

            stdout_bytes: 0,
            host_used: 0,
            host_oom: false,

            budget_hit: None,
```

```rust
/// Reports a host-side allocation over the memory limit exactly like a guest allocation failure.
fn host_oom(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, requested: usize) -> wasmtime::Error {
    if let Ok(mem) = memory(c) {
        let _ = mem.write(&mut *c, OOM_SCRATCH as usize, &(requested as i64).to_le_bytes());
    }
    c.data_mut().failure = Some(Failure { kind: FAIL_OOM, func: 0, site: 0, vals: 0 });
    wasmtime::Error::msg("out of memory")
}

```

with:

```rust
}

/// Reports a host-side allocation over the memory limit like a guest allocation failure, marked
/// as the host's.
pub fn host_oom(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, requested: usize) -> wasmtime::Error {
    if let Ok(mem) = memory(c) {
        let _ = mem.write(&mut *c, OOM_SCRATCH as usize, &(requested as i64).to_le_bytes());
    }
    c.data_mut().host_oom = true;
    c.data_mut().failure = Some(Failure { kind: FAIL_OOM, func: 0, site: 0, vals: 0 });
    wasmtime::Error::msg("out of memory")
}

/// Counts `bytes` against the execution's host budget (= its memory limit).
pub fn charge(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, bytes: usize) -> Result<(), wasmtime::Error> {
    let requested = c.data().host_used.saturating_add(bytes);
    if requested > c.data().limit {
        return Err(host_oom(c, requested));
    }
    c.data_mut().host_used = requested;
    Ok(())
}

/// Ends the execution with `budget_exceeded`.
fn budget_exceeded(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, kind: &'static str, budget: usize, used: usize) -> wasmtime::Error {
    c.data_mut().budget_hit = Some(BudgetHit { kind, budget, used });
    c.data_mut().failure = Some(Failure { kind: FAIL_BUDGET, func: 0, site: 0, vals: 0 });
    wasmtime::Error::msg("budget exceeded")
}
```

```rust
/// Keeps `line` of printed output: up to `stdout_limit` (the rest is only counted), or up to the
/// host budget (then `out_of_memory`).
fn print_line(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, line: &[u8]) -> Result<(), wasmtime::Error> {
    if let Some(keep) = c.data().stdout_limit {
        let data = c.data_mut();
        data.stdout_bytes = data.stdout_bytes.saturating_add(line.len());
        let room = keep.saturating_sub(data.stdout.len()).min(line.len());
        charge(c, room)?;
        c.data_mut().stdout.extend_from_slice(&line[..room]);
        return Ok(());
    }
    let room = c.data().limit.saturating_sub(c.data().host_used);
    if line.len() > room {
        c.data_mut().stdout.extend_from_slice(&line[..room]);
        let requested = c.data().host_used + line.len();
        return Err(host_oom(c, requested));
    }
    charge(c, line.len())?;
    c.data_mut().stdout.extend_from_slice(line);
    Ok(())
}

```

```rust
    }

    /// Writes an argument into guest memory (Str and List[...] are allocated) and returns its slot.
    fn encode(&self, store: &mut Store<HostState>, instance: &Instance, t: &Type, v: &Value) -> Result<i64, String> {
        Ok(match (t, v) {
            (Type::Int, Value::Int(n)) => *n,
            (Type::Float, Value::Float(x)) => x.to_bits() as i64,
            (Type::Bool, Value::Bool(b)) => *b as i64,
            (Type::Unit, Value::Unit) => 0,
            (Type::Str, Value::Str(s)) => {
                let p = self.alloc(store, instance, 4 + s.len() as i64)?;
                let mem = memory_of(store, instance)?;
                mem.write(&mut *store, p as usize, &(s.len() as u32).to_le_bytes()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                mem.write(&mut *store, p as usize + 4, s.as_bytes()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                p as i64
            }
            (Type::List(elem), Value::List(xs)) => {
                let mut slots = Vec::new();
                for x in xs {
                    slots.push(self.encode(store, instance, elem, x)?);
                }
                let p = self.alloc(store, instance, 8 + 8 * xs.len() as i64)?;
                let mem = memory_of(store, instance)?;
                mem.write(&mut *store, p as usize, &(xs.len() as u32).to_le_bytes()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                for (k, s) in slots.iter().enumerate() {
                    mem.write(&mut *store, p as usize + 8 + 8 * k, &s.to_le_bytes()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                }
                p as i64
            }
            (t, v) => return Err(format!("expected {t}, got {}", serde_json::to_string(v).unwrap_or_default())),
        })
    }

    fn alloc(&self, store: &mut Store<HostState>, instance: &Instance, size: i64) -> Result<i32, String> {
        let f = instance.get_typed_func::<i64, i32>(&mut *store, "qal_alloc").map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        f.call(&mut *store, size).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    }
```

```rust
                e.values = Values(vec![("requested_bytes".into(), Value::Int(r)), ("limit_bytes".into(), Value::Int(self.memory_limit as i64))]);
                if host {
                    e.values.0.push(("host".into(), Value::Bool(true)));
                }
            }
            return e;
        }
        if f.kind == FAIL_BUDGET {
            let Some(b) = store.data().budget_hit else {
                return RunError::new("trap", entry, "a budget was exceeded, but none is recorded");
            };
            let what = match b.kind {
                "fs_bytes" => format!("`write_file` may write at most {} bytes per execution", b.budget),
                "fs_writes" => format!("`write_file` may be called at most {} times per execution", b.budget),
                other => format!("the `{other}` budget of {} is used up", b.budget),
            };
            let mut e = RunError::new("budget_exceeded", entry, format!("budget exceeded: {what}"));
            e.values = Values(vec![("budget".into(), Value::Int(b.budget as i64)), ("used".into(), Value::Int(b.used as i64)), ("kind".into(), Value::Str(b.kind.into()))]);
            return e;
        }
```

```rust
/// How an effect is granted on the command line (`--allow <flag>`).
fn grant_flag(effect: &str) -> &str {
    match effect {
        "fs" => "fs:<dir>",
        "net" => "net:<host>",
        other => other,
    }
```

```rust
#[test]
fn databases_live_in_the_project_of_the_store() {
    let project = temp_dir().join("dbproject");
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&project);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(project.join("sub")).unwrap();
    let file = project.join("count.qal");
    std::fs::write(&file, "fn main(args: List[Str]) -> Int !db !io = match db_exec(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hits (n INTEGER)\", []) { Err(_) => 9, Ok(_) => match db_exec(\"INSERT INTO hits VALUES (1)\", []) { Err(_) => 8, Ok(_) => match db_query(\"SELECT count(*) FROM hits\", []) { Ok(r) => { print(join(map(r.columns, same), \",\")); match get(r.rows, 0) { Some(row) => match get(row, 0) { Some(SqlInt(n)) => if n == 2 then 3 else 0, _ => 7 }, None => 6 } }, Err(_) => 5 } } }\nfn same(s: Str) -> Str = s").unwrap();
    let run = |dir: &std::path::Path| {
        let out = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_qal")).current_dir(dir).args(["run", file.to_str().unwrap(), "--allow", "db,io"]).stdin(Stdio::null()).output().unwrap();
        (out.status.code().unwrap(), serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&out.stdout).unwrap())
    };
    assert_eq!(qal_json_in(&project, &["init"]).0, 0);
    assert_eq!(run(&project.join("sub")), (0, json!({"exit_code": 0, "stdout": "count(*)\n", "db": "commit"})));
    assert!(project.join(".qal/db/main.sqlite").is_file(), "next to the store, found from a subdirectory");
    // The second run sees 2 rows and exits 3: its insert is rolled back.
    assert_eq!(run(&project), (3, json!({"exit_code": 3, "stdout": "count(*)\n", "db": "rollback"})));
    assert_eq!(run(&project).0, 3, "still 2 rows: the rolled back insert is gone");
}

fn qal_json_in(dir: &std::path::Path, args: &[&str]) -> (i32, Value) {
    let out = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_qal")).current_dir(dir).args(args).stdin(Stdio::null()).output().unwrap();
    (out.status.code().unwrap(), serde_json::from_slice(&out.stdout).unwrap())
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn agents_use_an_in_memory_database_or_the_projects_file() {
    let mut c = Client::start();
    c.initialize();
    let src = "fn setup() -> Result[Int, DbError] !db = db_exec(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notes (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, body TEXT NOT NULL)\", [])\n\
               fn note(b: Str) -> Int !db = match setup() { Ok(_) => match db_exec(\"INSERT INTO notes (body) VALUES (?)\", [SqlText(b)]) { Ok(n) => n, Err(_) => 0 - 1 }, Err(_) => 0 - 2 }\n\
               fn notes() -> Int !db = match db_query(\"SELECT count(*) FROM notes\", []) { Ok(r) => len(r.rows), Err(DbSql(m)) => 0 - len(m), Err(_) => 0 - 1 }";
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_define", json!({"source": src}));
    assert!(!err, "{v}");
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_call", json!({"fn": "note", "args": ["x"]}));
    assert_eq!((err, v["error"]["hints"].clone()), (true, json!(["pass `\"allow\": [\"db:mem\"]` to qal_run or qal_call"])));
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_call", json!({"fn": "note", "args": ["x"], "allow": ["db:mem"]}));
    assert_eq!((err, v), (false, json!({"ok": 1, "stdout": "", "db": "commit"})));
    assert!(!c.root.join(".qal/db").exists(), "db:mem leaves no file");
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_call", json!({"fn": "note", "args": ["y"], "allow": ["db"]}));
    assert_eq!((err, v["ok"].clone()), (false, json!(1)));
    assert!(c.root.join(".qal/db/main.sqlite").is_file(), "`db` is the project's .qal/db/main.sqlite");
    let (_, v) = c.call("qal_call", json!({"fn": "notes", "allow": ["db"]}));
    assert_eq!(v["ok"], json!(1));
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_call", json!({"fn": "notes", "allow": ["db", "db:mem"]}));
    assert!(err && v["diagnostics"][0]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("only one database"), "{v}");
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_define", json!({"source": "fn bad(t: Str) -> Result[Int, DbError] !db = db_exec(\"DELETE FROM notes WHERE body = '\" ++ t ++ \"'\", [])"}));
    assert_eq!((err, v["diagnostics"][0]["code"].as_str()), (true, Some("E0241")));
}
```

```rust
/// A fresh project directory for database tests.
fn db_project(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("qal-run-db-{name}-{}", std::process::id()));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
    dir
}

const TASKS_DB: &str = "fn setup() -> Result[Int, DbError] !db = db_exec(\"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS todos (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, title TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, done INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)\", [])\n\
    fn add(t: Str) -> Int !db = match db_query(\"INSERT INTO todos (title) VALUES (?) RETURNING id\", [SqlText(t)]) { Ok(r) => match get(r.rows, 0) { Some(row) => match get(row, 0) { Some(SqlInt(id)) => id, _ => 0 - 2 }, None => 0 - 3 }, Err(DbConstraint(_)) => 0 - 1, Err(_) => 0 - 4 }\n\
    fn count() -> Int !db = match db_query(\"SELECT count(*) AS n, ? AS echo FROM todos\", [SqlText(\"x'); DROP TABLE todos; --\")]) { Ok(r) => match get(r.rows, 0) { Some(row) => match get(row, 0) { Some(SqlInt(n)) => n, _ => 0 - 2 }, None => 0 - 3 }, Err(_) => 0 - 4 }\n\
    fn cols() -> List[Str] !db = match db_query(\"SELECT id, title AS name FROM todos\", []) { Ok(r) => r.columns, Err(_) => [] }\n\
    fn main(args: List[Str]) -> Int !db = { let s = setup() in match get(args, 0) { Some(\"add\") => match get(args, 1) { Some(t) => if add(t) > 0 then 0 else 1, None => 2 }, Some(\"fail\") => { let x = add(\"temp\") in 3 }, Some(\"trap\") => { let x = add(\"temp\") in 1 / (x - x) }, _ => count() } }";

#[test]
fn db_runs_parameterized_statements_with_one_transaction_per_execution() {
    let project = db_project("tx");
    let run = |args: &[&str]| {
        let p = Permissions::parse("db:todo").unwrap().with_db_dir(project.join(".qal/db"));
        let out = main_runner(TASKS_DB, "").with_permissions(p).run_main(&args.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect::<Vec<_>>());
        (out.result.map_err(|e| e.kind), out.db)
    };
    assert_eq!(run(&["add", "a"]), (Ok(Value::Int(0)), "commit"));
    assert_eq!(run(&["add", "b"]), (Ok(Value::Int(0)), "commit"));
    assert_eq!(run(&["add", "a"]), (Ok(Value::Int(1)), "rollback"), "unique constraint: Err(DbConstraint), exit 1");
    // Exit code != 0 and traps roll the execution's writes back.
    assert_eq!(run(&["fail"]), (Ok(Value::Int(3)), "rollback"));
    assert_eq!(run(&["trap"]), (Err("div_by_zero"), "rollback"));
    assert_eq!(run(&[]), (Ok(Value::Int(2)), "rollback"), "two rows; exit code 2 also rolls back");
    assert!(project.join(".qal/db/todo.sqlite").is_file());
    // `call` commits unless there is a runtime error; values written as parameters stay data.
    let p = Permissions::parse("db:todo").unwrap().with_db_dir(project.join(".qal/db"));
    let r = Runner::new(compile(TASKS_DB, Entry::All)).unwrap().with_permissions(p);
    assert_eq!(r.call("count", &[]), Ok(Value::Int(2)));
    let out = r.invoke("add", &[Value::Str("c".into())]);
    assert_eq!((out.result, out.db), (Ok(Value::Int(3)), "commit"));
    assert_eq!(r.call("cols", &[]), Ok(Value::List(vec![Value::Str("id".into()), Value::Str("name".into())])));
    let out = r.invoke("main", &[Value::List(vec![])]);
    assert_eq!((out.result, out.db), (Ok(Value::Int(3)), "commit"), "invoke commits on success; only run_entry needs exit code 0");
    // A function without `db` opens no database.
    assert_eq!(main_runner("fn main(args: List[Str]) -> Int = 0", "").with_permissions(Permissions::parse("db").unwrap()).run_main(&[]).db, "none");
}

#[test]
fn db_mem_is_fresh_per_execution_and_db_needs_a_grant() {
    let r = main_runner(TASKS_DB, "db:mem");
    assert_eq!(r.run_main(&["add".into(), "a".into()]).result, Ok(Value::Int(0)));
    assert_eq!(r.run_main(&[]).result, Ok(Value::Int(0)), "a fresh in-memory database: no rows");
    let r = Runner::new(compile(TASKS_DB, Entry::All)).unwrap().with_permissions(Permissions::parse("db:mem").unwrap());
    assert_eq!(r.call("add", &[Value::Str("a".into())]), Ok(Value::Int(-4)), "no table: Err(DbSql(..))");
    let e = main_runner(TASKS_DB, "io").run_main(&[]).result.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, e.hints.clone()), ("permission_denied", vec!["grant them with `--allow db`".to_string()]));
    assert!(Permissions::parse("db,db:mem").unwrap_err().contains("only one database"));
    assert!(Permissions::parse("db:Bad").unwrap_err().contains("a database name"));
    let root = db_project("confined");
    let p = Permissions::parse_in(&["db".to_string()], &root).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(p.db_dir(), root.canonicalize().unwrap().join(".qal/db"));
}

#[test]
fn db_files_that_are_links_or_not_databases_are_db_open_errors() {
    let project = db_project("open");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(project.join(".qal/db")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(project.join(".qal/db/main.sqlite"), "not a database file at all, just text").unwrap();
    let p = Permissions::parse("db").unwrap().with_db_dir(project.join(".qal/db"));
    let e = main_runner(TASKS_DB, "").with_permissions(p).run_main(&[]).result.unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, e.code), ("db_open", "E0523"));
}

#[test]
fn db_results_count_against_the_memory_limit_and_rows_are_bounded() {
    let src = "fn big(n: Int) -> Int !db = match db_query(\"WITH RECURSIVE c(x) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT x + 1 FROM c WHERE x < ?) SELECT x, printf('%.1000c', 'a') FROM c\", [SqlInt(n)]) { Ok(r) => len(r.rows), Err(DbTooLarge) => 0 - 1, Err(_) => 0 - 2 }";
    let r = Runner::new(compile(src, Entry::All)).unwrap().with_permissions(Permissions::parse("db:mem").unwrap()).with_memory_limit(4 << 20);
    assert_eq!(r.call("big", &[Value::Int(100)]), Ok(Value::Int(100)));
    let e = r.call("big", &[Value::Int(9000)]).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(e.kind, "out_of_memory", "9 MB of results exceed the 4 MiB host budget");
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&e.values).unwrap()["host"], json!(true));
    let r = Runner::new(compile(src, Entry::All)).unwrap().with_permissions(Permissions::parse("db:mem").unwrap()).with_limits(Limits { db_max_rows: 50, ..Limits::default() });
    assert_eq!(r.call("big", &[Value::Int(51)]), Ok(Value::Int(-1)));
}
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn exact_decoding_never_cuts_and_bounds_the_size() {
        let tp = TProgram::default();
        let mut mem = vec![0u8; 64];
        mem[0..4].copy_from_slice(&5u32.to_le_bytes());
        mem[4..9].copy_from_slice(b"hello");
        mem[16..20].copy_from_slice(&2u32.to_le_bytes());
        mem[24..32].copy_from_slice(&0i64.to_le_bytes());
        mem[32..40].copy_from_slice(&0i64.to_le_bytes());
        let list = Type::list(Type::Str);
        assert_eq!(decode_exact(&tp, &mem, &list, 16, 1000), Ok(Value::List(vec![Value::Str("hello".into()), Value::Str("hello".into())])));
        assert_eq!(decode_exact(&tp, &mem, &list, 16, 20).unwrap_err(), "the value is larger than 20 bytes");
        mem[4] = 0xff;
        assert!(decode_exact(&tp, &mem, &Type::Str, 0, 1000).unwrap_err().contains("not valid UTF-8"));
        assert!(decode_exact(&tp, &mem, &Type::Str, 1 << 20, 1000).unwrap_err().starts_with("invalid Str"));
        assert!(decode_exact(&tp, &mem, &Type::option(Type::Int), 4, 1000).is_err(), "tag out of range");
    }
}
```

Create `qal/crates/qal-run/src/db.rs`:

```rust
//! The `db` effect: SQLite through the host (Phase 3 spec §3.2, §5.2).
//!
//! The guest never chooses a file: the host opens `<project>/.qal/db/<name>.sqlite` (or an
//! in-memory database) and runs only the SQL literals of the program, with positional parameters,
//! one statement at a time, under an authorizer that refuses attaching, pragmas, transaction
//! control, extension functions and schema-table writes. The host owns the one transaction per
//! execution (`BEGIN IMMEDIATE` before the entry point, commit or rollback after it).

use rusqlite::hooks::{AuthAction, AuthContext, Authorization};
use rusqlite::types::ValueRef;
use rusqlite::{Connection, ErrorCode, OpenFlags};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::Duration;

/// Where the database of an execution lives.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum DbGrant {
    /// `db`: `<project>/.qal/db/main.sqlite`
    Main,
    /// `db:<name>`: `<project>/.qal/db/<name>.sqlite`
    Named(String),
    /// `db:mem`: a fresh in-memory database
    Memory,
}

impl DbGrant {
    /// Parses the part after `db` in a grant: ``, `:mem` or `:<name>`.
    pub fn parse(item: &str) -> Result<DbGrant, String> {
        match item.strip_prefix("db") {
            Some("") => Ok(DbGrant::Main),
            Some(":mem") => Ok(DbGrant::Memory),
            Some(rest) => {
                let name = rest.strip_prefix(':').unwrap_or("");
                let valid = name.len() <= 32 && name.starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_lowercase()) && name.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
                if valid {
                    Ok(DbGrant::Named(name.to_string()))
                } else {
                    Err(format!("`{item}`: a database name is 1 to 32 characters `a-z`, `0-9`, `_`, starting with a letter (`db`, `db:<name>` or `db:mem`)"))
                }
            }
            None => Err(format!("`{item}` is not a database grant")),
        }
    }

    /// The grant as written.
    pub fn spec(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            DbGrant::Main => "db".into(),
            DbGrant::Named(n) => format!("db:{n}"),
            DbGrant::Memory => "db:mem".into(),
        }
    }

    /// The database file below `dir` (None for `db:mem`).
    pub fn file(&self, dir: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        match self {
            DbGrant::Main => Some(dir.join("main.sqlite")),
            DbGrant::Named(n) => Some(dir.join(format!("{n}.sqlite"))),
            DbGrant::Memory => None,
        }
    }
}

/// A value of a parameter or a result cell (`SqlValue` in QAL).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum SqlVal {
    Null,
    Int(i64),
    Float(f64),
    Text(String),
}

/// A failed statement (`DbError` in QAL), or the host budget exhausted while reading results.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum DbErr {
    Constraint(String),
    Sql(String),
    Busy,
    Full,
    TooLarge,
    /// More result bytes than the host budget has left (the execution ends with `out_of_memory`).
    HostOom(usize),
}

/// Rows of a query: column names and values.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Rows {
    pub columns: Vec<String>,
    pub rows: Vec<Vec<SqlVal>>,
    /// Host bytes the result holds (strings plus 8 per value).
    pub bytes: usize,
}

/// How long a statement waits for another connection's write lock.
pub const BUSY_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);

/// SQLite page size of new databases (`max_page_count` is computed from it).
const PAGE_SIZE: u64 = 4096;

/// Functions the guest's SQL may not call (most do not exist in the library build; listed so a
/// future build cannot quietly add them).
const FORBIDDEN_FUNCTIONS: [&str; 5] = ["load_extension", "readfile", "writefile", "fts3_tokenizer", "edit"];

/// The authorizer for guest statements. Writes to the schema table cannot be told apart from the
/// ones CREATE/ALTER/DROP make internally (same actions, no accessor), so they are left to SQLite:
/// without `writable_schema` (a pragma, refused) and in defensive mode it refuses direct writes.
fn authorize(ctx: AuthContext<'_>) -> Authorization {
    match ctx.action {
        AuthAction::Attach { .. } | AuthAction::Detach { .. } | AuthAction::Pragma { .. } | AuthAction::Transaction { .. } | AuthAction::Savepoint { .. } => Authorization::Deny,
        AuthAction::Function { function_name } if FORBIDDEN_FUNCTIONS.iter().any(|f| f.eq_ignore_ascii_case(function_name)) => Authorization::Deny,
        AuthAction::Unknown { .. } => Authorization::Deny,
        _ => Authorization::Allow,
    }
}

fn sql_err(e: rusqlite::Error) -> DbErr {
    match &e {
        rusqlite::Error::SqliteFailure(f, msg) => {
            let m = msg.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| e.to_string());
            match f.code {
                ErrorCode::ConstraintViolation => DbErr::Constraint(m),
                ErrorCode::DatabaseBusy | ErrorCode::DatabaseLocked => DbErr::Busy,
                ErrorCode::DiskFull => DbErr::Full,
                _ => DbErr::Sql(m),
            }
        }
        rusqlite::Error::MultipleStatement => DbErr::Sql("only one statement is allowed".into()),
        rusqlite::Error::ExecuteReturnedResults => DbErr::Sql("the statement returns rows: use db_query".into()),
        _ => DbErr::Sql(e.to_string()),
    }
}

/// One execution's (or one worker's) database connection.
pub struct Db {
    conn: Connection,
    in_tx: bool,
}

impl Db {
    /// Opens the database of `grant` below `dir` (created if needed; the file must not be a
    /// symbolic link) with the host's settings; `max_mib` bounds its size.
    pub fn open(grant: &DbGrant, dir: &Path, max_mib: usize) -> Result<Db, String> {
        let conn = match grant.file(dir) {
            None => Connection::open_in_memory().map_err(|e| format!("cannot open an in-memory database: {e}"))?,
            Some(file) => {
                std::fs::create_dir_all(dir).map_err(|e| format!("cannot create {}: {e}", dir.display()))?;
                if std::fs::symlink_metadata(dir).map(|m| m.file_type().is_symlink()).unwrap_or(false) {
                    return Err(format!("{} is a symbolic link", dir.display()));
                }
                // SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW refuses a link anywhere in the path: open by the real path,
                // so only a link at the file itself is refused.
                let real = dir.canonicalize().map_err(|e| format!("cannot open {}: {e}", dir.display()))?;
                let file = real.join(file.file_name().unwrap_or_default());
                if std::fs::symlink_metadata(&file).map(|m| m.file_type().is_symlink()).unwrap_or(false) {
                    return Err(format!("{} is a symbolic link", file.display()));
                }
                let flags = OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_WRITE | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_NO_MUTEX;
                Connection::open_with_flags(&file, flags).map_err(|e| format!("cannot open {}: {e}", file.display()))?
            }
        };
        let setup = |c: &Connection| -> rusqlite::Result<()> {
            c.busy_timeout(BUSY_TIMEOUT)?;
            c.set_db_config(rusqlite::config::DbConfig::SQLITE_DBCONFIG_DEFENSIVE, true)?;
            c.set_db_config(rusqlite::config::DbConfig::SQLITE_DBCONFIG_TRUSTED_SCHEMA, false)?;
            c.pragma_update(None, "foreign_keys", true)?;
            c.pragma_update(None, "trusted_schema", false)?;
            c.pragma_update(None, "cell_size_check", true)?;
            c.pragma_update(None, "page_size", PAGE_SIZE as i64)?;
            let _: String = c.query_row("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL", [], |r| r.get(0))?;
            let pages = (max_mib as u64).saturating_mul(1 << 20) / PAGE_SIZE;
            let _: i64 = c.query_row(&format!("PRAGMA max_page_count = {}", pages.max(1)), [], |r| r.get(0))?;
            // Fails early on a file that is not a database.
            let _: i64 = c.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_schema", [], |r| r.get(0))?;
            Ok(())
        };
        setup(&conn).map_err(|e| format!("cannot set up the database: {e}"))?;
        Ok(Db { conn, in_tx: false })
    }

    /// Starts the execution's transaction (`BEGIN IMMEDIATE`: writers serialize).
    pub fn begin(&mut self) -> Result<(), DbErr> {
        self.conn.execute_batch("BEGIN IMMEDIATE").map_err(sql_err)?;
        self.in_tx = true;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Ends the transaction: commits if `commit` (falling back to a rollback if that fails).
    /// Returns what happened: `commit` or `rollback`.
    pub fn finish(&mut self, commit: bool) -> Result<&'static str, String> {
        if !self.in_tx {
            return Ok("none");
        }
        self.in_tx = false;
        // SQLite rolls a transaction back by itself after some errors (SQLITE_FULL, ...).
        if self.conn.is_autocommit() {
            return if commit { Err("the transaction was rolled back by the database after an error".into()) } else { Ok("rollback") };
        }
        if commit {
            match self.conn.execute_batch("COMMIT") {
                Ok(()) => return Ok("commit"),
                Err(e) => {
                    let _ = self.conn.execute_batch("ROLLBACK");
                    return Err(format!("the transaction could not be committed: {e}"));
                }
            }
        }
        self.conn.execute_batch("ROLLBACK").map_err(|e| format!("the transaction could not be rolled back: {e}"))?;
        Ok("rollback")
    }

    /// Prepares a guest statement under the authorizer and binds its parameters.
    fn prepare<'c>(&'c self, sql: &str, params: &[SqlVal]) -> Result<rusqlite::Statement<'c>, DbErr> {
        qal_core::sql::check_statement(sql).map_err(DbErr::Sql)?;
        if self.in_tx && self.conn.is_autocommit() {
            // Running on would write outside the execution's transaction.
            return Err(DbErr::Sql("the transaction of this execution was rolled back after an earlier error; no further statements run".into()));
        }
        self.conn.authorizer(Some(authorize)).map_err(sql_err)?;
        let prepared = self.conn.prepare(sql);
        // Statements can be re-prepared on schema changes while they run: keep the authorizer
        // until the statement is finished (`guarded` below clears it).
        let mut stmt = match prepared {
            Ok(s) => s,
            Err(e) => {
                let _ = self.conn.authorizer(None::<fn(AuthContext<'_>) -> Authorization>);
                return Err(sql_err(e));
            }
        };
        let expected = stmt.parameter_count();
        if expected != params.len() {
            let _ = self.conn.authorizer(None::<fn(AuthContext<'_>) -> Authorization>);
            return Err(DbErr::Sql(format!("expected {expected} parameters, got {}", params.len())));
        }
        for (i, p) in params.iter().enumerate() {
            let r = match p {
                SqlVal::Null => stmt.raw_bind_parameter(i + 1, rusqlite::types::Null),
                SqlVal::Int(n) => stmt.raw_bind_parameter(i + 1, n),
                SqlVal::Float(x) => stmt.raw_bind_parameter(i + 1, x),
                SqlVal::Text(s) => stmt.raw_bind_parameter(i + 1, s.as_str()),
            };
            if let Err(e) = r {
                let _ = self.conn.authorizer(None::<fn(AuthContext<'_>) -> Authorization>);
                return Err(sql_err(e));
            }
        }
        Ok(stmt)
    }

    fn unguard(&self) {
        let _ = self.conn.authorizer(None::<fn(AuthContext<'_>) -> Authorization>);
    }

    /// `db_exec`: runs a statement that returns no rows; the number of changed rows.
    pub fn exec(&self, sql: &str, params: &[SqlVal]) -> Result<i64, DbErr> {
        let before = self.conn.total_changes();
        let mut stmt = self.prepare(sql, params)?;
        let r = stmt.raw_execute();
        drop(stmt);
        self.unguard();
        r.map_err(sql_err)?;
        // `changes()` keeps the count of the last INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE; other statements change nothing.
        Ok(if self.conn.total_changes() == before { 0 } else { self.conn.changes() as i64 })
    }

    /// `db_query`: at most `max_rows` rows and `room` bytes of results.
    pub fn query(&self, sql: &str, params: &[SqlVal], max_rows: usize, room: usize) -> Result<Rows, DbErr> {
        let mut stmt = self.prepare(sql, params)?;
        let r = Self::read_rows(&mut stmt, max_rows, room);
        drop(stmt);
        self.unguard();
        r
    }

    fn read_rows(stmt: &mut rusqlite::Statement<'_>, max_rows: usize, room: usize) -> Result<Rows, DbErr> {
        let columns: Vec<String> = stmt.column_names().into_iter().map(str::to_string).collect();
        let mut bytes: usize = columns.iter().map(|c| c.len() + 8).sum();
        let n = columns.len();
        let mut rows = Vec::new();
        let mut it = stmt.raw_query();
        while let Some(row) = it.next().map_err(sql_err)? {
            if rows.len() == max_rows {
                return Err(DbErr::TooLarge);
            }
            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(n);
            for i in 0..n {
                let v = match row.get_ref(i).map_err(sql_err)? {
                    ValueRef::Null => SqlVal::Null,
                    ValueRef::Integer(k) => SqlVal::Int(k),
                    ValueRef::Real(x) => SqlVal::Float(x),
                    ValueRef::Text(t) => {
                        bytes = bytes.saturating_add(t.len());
                        if bytes > room {
                            return Err(DbErr::HostOom(bytes));
                        }
                        SqlVal::Text(String::from_utf8(t.to_vec()).map_err(|_| DbErr::Sql(format!("column `{}` holds text that is not UTF-8", columns[i])))?)
                    }
                    ValueRef::Blob(_) => return Err(DbErr::Sql("BLOB columns are not supported".into())),
                };
                out.push(v);
            }
            bytes = bytes.saturating_add(8 * n + 8);
            if bytes > room {
                return Err(DbErr::HostOom(bytes));
            }
            rows.push(out);
        }
        Ok(Rows { columns, rows, bytes })
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn mem() -> Db {
        Db::open(&DbGrant::Memory, Path::new("/nonexistent"), 1024).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn grants_parse_and_name_files() {
        assert_eq!(DbGrant::parse("db"), Ok(DbGrant::Main));
        assert_eq!(DbGrant::parse("db:mem"), Ok(DbGrant::Memory));
        assert_eq!(DbGrant::parse("db:todo_2"), Ok(DbGrant::Named("todo_2".into())));
        for bad in ["db:", "db:Todo", "db:2x", "db:../x", "db:a-b", "db:abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzabcdefg", "dbx"] {
            assert!(DbGrant::parse(bad).is_err(), "{bad}");
        }
        assert_eq!(DbGrant::Named("t".into()).file(Path::new("/p/.qal/db")), Some(PathBuf::from("/p/.qal/db/t.sqlite")));
        assert_eq!(DbGrant::Memory.file(Path::new("/p")), None);
        assert_eq!(DbGrant::Named("t".into()).spec(), "db:t");
    }

    #[test]
    fn statements_run_with_positional_parameters() {
        let db = mem();
        assert_eq!(db.exec("CREATE TABLE t (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, title TEXT NOT NULL, score REAL, note TEXT)", &[]), Ok(0));
        assert_eq!(db.exec("INSERT INTO t (title, score, note) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", &[SqlVal::Text("a'; DROP TABLE t; --".into()), SqlVal::Float(1.5), SqlVal::Null]), Ok(1));
        assert_eq!(db.exec("INSERT INTO t (title) VALUES (?)", &[SqlVal::Text("b".into())]), Ok(1));
        let r = db.query("SELECT id, title, score, note FROM t ORDER BY id", &[], 100, 1 << 20).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.columns, vec!["id", "title", "score", "note"]);
        assert_eq!(r.rows[0], vec![SqlVal::Int(1), SqlVal::Text("a'; DROP TABLE t; --".into()), SqlVal::Float(1.5), SqlVal::Null]);
        assert_eq!(r.rows.len(), 2);
        let r = db.query("INSERT INTO t (title) VALUES (?) RETURNING id", &[SqlVal::Text("c".into())], 100, 1 << 20).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.rows, vec![vec![SqlVal::Int(3)]]);
        assert_eq!(db.exec("SELECT 1", &[]), Err(DbErr::Sql("the statement returns rows: use db_query".into())));
        assert_eq!(db.exec("INSERT INTO t (title) VALUES (?)", &[]), Err(DbErr::Sql("expected 1 parameters, got 0".into())));
        assert!(matches!(db.exec("INSERT INTO t (id, title) VALUES (1, 'dup')", &[]), Err(DbErr::Constraint(_))));
        assert!(matches!(db.exec("INSERT INTO t (title) VALUES (NULL)", &[]), Err(DbErr::Constraint(_))));
        assert!(matches!(db.query("SELECT nosuch FROM t", &[], 10, 1 << 20), Err(DbErr::Sql(_))));
        assert_eq!(db.query("SELECT x'00'", &[], 10, 1 << 20), Err(DbErr::Sql("BLOB columns are not supported".into())));
        assert_eq!(db.query("SELECT CAST(x'ff' AS TEXT)", &[], 10, 1 << 20), Err(DbErr::Sql("column `CAST(x'ff' AS TEXT)` holds text that is not UTF-8".into())));
        assert_eq!(db.query("WITH RECURSIVE c(x) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT x + 1 FROM c WHERE x < 11) SELECT x FROM c", &[], 10, 1 << 20).err(), Some(DbErr::TooLarge));
        assert!(matches!(db.query("SELECT title FROM t", &[], 10, 10), Err(DbErr::HostOom(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_authorizer_refuses_control_statements_and_functions() {
        let db = mem();
        db.exec("CREATE TABLE t (a TEXT)", &[]).unwrap();
        db.exec("CREATE INDEX t_a ON t (a)", &[]).unwrap();
        db.exec("CREATE VIEW v AS SELECT a FROM t", &[]).unwrap();
        db.exec("CREATE TRIGGER tr AFTER INSERT ON t BEGIN UPDATE t SET a = upper(new.a) WHERE rowid = new.rowid; END", &[]).unwrap();
        db.exec("INSERT INTO t VALUES ('x')", &[]).unwrap();
        db.exec("DROP VIEW v", &[]).unwrap();
        db.exec("ALTER TABLE t ADD COLUMN b INTEGER", &[]).unwrap();
        let refused = |sql: &str| match db.exec(sql, &[]) {
            Err(DbErr::Sql(m)) => m,
            other => panic!("{sql}: {other:?}"),
        };
        // The static check refuses these first; the authorizer is the second line.
        for sql in ["ATTACH 'x.db' AS x", "PRAGMA writable_schema = 1", "BEGIN", "SAVEPOINT a", "VACUUM"] {
            assert!(refused(sql).contains("not allowed"), "{sql}");
        }
        assert!(refused("SELECT load_extension('x')").contains("not authorized") || refused("SELECT load_extension('x')").contains("no such function"));
        assert!(refused("UPDATE sqlite_master SET sql = 'x'").contains("may not be modified"));
        assert!(refused("DELETE FROM sqlite_schema").contains("may not be modified"));
        assert!(refused("INSERT INTO sqlite_master VALUES ('table', 'x', 'x', 0, 'CREATE TABLE x (a)')").contains("may not be modified"));
        assert!(refused("SELECT * FROM pragma_table_info('t')").contains("not authorized"), "table-valued pragmas are pragmas");
        assert!(refused("SELECT 1; SELECT 2").contains("only one statement"));
        // The host's own statements are not affected.
        let mut db = db;
        db.begin().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(db.finish(true), Ok("commit"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn transactions_commit_or_roll_back() {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("qal-db-tx-{}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        let grant = DbGrant::Named("t".into());
        let mut db = Db::open(&grant, &dir, 16).unwrap();
        db.begin().unwrap();
        db.exec("CREATE TABLE t (a INTEGER)", &[]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(db.finish(true), Ok("commit"));
        db.begin().unwrap();
        db.exec("INSERT INTO t VALUES (1)", &[]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(db.finish(false), Ok("rollback"));
        assert_eq!(db.finish(true), Ok("none"));
        let other = Db::open(&grant, &dir, 16).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(other.query("SELECT count(*) FROM t", &[], 10, 1 << 20).unwrap().rows, vec![vec![SqlVal::Int(0)]]);
        assert!(dir.join("t.sqlite").exists());
        // A writer blocks another writer's BEGIN IMMEDIATE (busy after the timeout).
        db.begin().unwrap();
        let mut second = Db::open(&grant, &dir, 16).unwrap();
        second.conn.busy_timeout(Duration::from_millis(50)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(second.begin(), Err(DbErr::Busy));
        db.finish(false).unwrap();
        // The size limit: max_page_count makes the database full.
        let mut small = Db::open(&DbGrant::Named("small".into()), &dir, 1).unwrap();
        small.begin().unwrap();
        small.exec("CREATE TABLE b (s TEXT)", &[]).unwrap();
        let big = SqlVal::Text("x".repeat(300_000));
        let mut full = false;
        for _ in 0..10 {
            if small.exec("INSERT INTO b VALUES (?)", std::slice::from_ref(&big)) == Err(DbErr::Full) {
                full = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        assert!(full, "1 MiB limit reached");
        assert!(matches!(small.exec("INSERT INTO b VALUES ('y')", &[]), Err(DbErr::Sql(m)) if m.contains("rolled back")), "nothing runs outside the transaction");
        assert_eq!(small.finish(false), Ok("rollback"));
        small.begin().unwrap();
        assert!(small.finish(true).is_ok());
    }

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn database_files_are_never_symlinks() {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("qal-db-link-{}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        let target = dir.join("elsewhere.sqlite");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&target, dir.join("main.sqlite")).unwrap();
        assert!(Db::open(&DbGrant::Main, &dir, 16).err().unwrap().contains("symbolic link"));
        assert!(!target.exists());
        std::fs::write(dir.join("junk.sqlite"), "this is not a database, just some text").unwrap();
        assert!(Db::open(&DbGrant::Named("junk".into()), &dir, 16).is_err());
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// `.qal`, `.claude`, `.env`, ... inside a granted project directory).
    pub no_hidden: bool,
    /// The database grant (`db`, `db:<name>`, `db:mem`); at most one per execution.
    pub db: Option<DbGrant>,
    /// Where database files live (`<project>/.qal/db`); None: `.qal/db` below the working directory.
    pub db_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
}

/// Known grants, for error messages.
const KNOWN_GRANTS: &str = "io, time, rand, fs:<dir>, db, db:<name>, db:mem";

/// Adds a database grant (only one per execution).
fn grant_db(p: &mut Permissions, item: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    let g = DbGrant::parse(item)?;
    if let Some(old) = &p.db {
        return Err(format!("only one database can be granted per execution: `{}` and `{item}`", old.spec()));
    }
    p.db = Some(g);
    Ok(())
}

fn is_db_item(item: &str) -> bool {
    item == "db" || item.starts_with("db:")
}
```

```rust
/// The prelude types: everything host functions read and write is built from them.
pub fn prelude_program() -> &'static TProgram {
    static PRELUDE: std::sync::OnceLock<TProgram> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
    PRELUDE.get_or_init(|| TProgram { types: qal_core::prelude::types(), ..TProgram::default() })
}

/// The guest's memory and allocator, from inside a host function.
fn guest(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>) -> Result<Guest, wasmtime::Error> {
    let mem = memory(c)?;
    let alloc = c.get_export("qal_alloc").and_then(Extern::into_func).ok_or_else(|| wasmtime::Error::msg("no qal_alloc"))?;
    Ok(Guest::new(mem, alloc.typed::<i64, i32>(&c)?))
}

/// Writes a host-built value of a prelude-based type into guest memory; returns the pointer.
fn write_value(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, ty: &Type, v: &Value) -> Result<i32, wasmtime::Error> {
    let g = guest(c)?;
    match g.encode(c, prelude_program(), ty, v) {
        Ok(slot) => Ok(slot as i32),
        Err(crate::guest::EncodeError::Trap(e)) => Err(e),
        Err(crate::guest::EncodeError::Mismatch(m)) => Err(wasmtime::Error::msg(format!("internal error: a host value does not match its type: {m}"))),
    }
}

/// Reads a value of a prelude-based type from guest memory, exactly.
fn read_value(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, ty: &Type, ptr: i32) -> Result<Value, wasmtime::Error> {
    let mem = memory(c)?;
    let max = c.data().limit;
    decode_exact(prelude_program(), mem.data(&c), ty, ptr as u32 as i64, max).map_err(|e| wasmtime::Error::msg(format!("cannot read the argument: {e}")))
}

fn named(n: &str) -> Type {
    Type::Named(n.to_string())
}

fn ok(v: Value) -> Value {
    Value::Variant("Ok".into(), vec![v])
}

fn err(v: Value) -> Value {
    Value::Variant("Err".into(), vec![v])
}

fn variant(name: &str, payload: Vec<Value>) -> Value {
    Value::Variant(name.into(), payload)
}

/// SQL parameters from a guest `List[SqlValue]`.
fn sql_params(v: Value) -> Result<Vec<SqlVal>, wasmtime::Error> {
    let Value::List(xs) = v else { return Err(wasmtime::Error::msg("internal error: parameters are not a list")) };
    xs.into_iter()
        .map(|x| match x {
            Value::Variant(n, p) => Ok(match (n.as_str(), p.as_slice()) {
                ("SqlNull", []) => SqlVal::Null,
                ("SqlInt", [Value::Int(k)]) => SqlVal::Int(*k),
                ("SqlFloat", [Value::Float(f)]) => SqlVal::Float(*f),
                ("SqlText", [Value::Str(s)]) => SqlVal::Text(s.clone()),
                _ => return Err(wasmtime::Error::msg("internal error: malformed SqlValue")),
            }),
            _ => Err(wasmtime::Error::msg("internal error: malformed SqlValue")),
        })
        .collect()
}

fn sql_value(v: SqlVal) -> Value {
    match v {
        SqlVal::Null => variant("SqlNull", vec![]),
        SqlVal::Int(n) => variant("SqlInt", vec![Value::Int(n)]),
        SqlVal::Float(x) => variant("SqlFloat", vec![Value::Float(x)]),
        SqlVal::Text(s) => variant("SqlText", vec![Value::Str(s)]),
    }
}

/// A `DbError` value, or the host OOM trap for results larger than the budget.
fn db_error(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, e: DbErr) -> Result<Value, wasmtime::Error> {
    Ok(match e {
        DbErr::Constraint(m) => variant("DbConstraint", vec![Value::Str(m)]),
        DbErr::Sql(m) => variant("DbSql", vec![Value::Str(m)]),
        DbErr::Busy => variant("DbBusy", vec![]),
        DbErr::Full => variant("DbFull", vec![]),
        DbErr::TooLarge => variant("DbTooLarge", vec![]),
        DbErr::HostOom(bytes) => {
            let requested = c.data().host_used.saturating_add(bytes);
            return Err(host_oom(c, requested));
        }
    })
}

/// Runs `db_exec` / `db_query` for the guest: reads the SQL and parameters, runs the statement on
/// the execution's connection and writes the `Result` into guest memory.
fn db_call(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, sql: i32, params: i32, query: bool) -> Result<i32, wasmtime::Error> {
    let sql = guest_str(c, sql)?;
    let params = sql_params(read_value(c, &Type::list(named("SqlValue")), params)?)?;
    let (max_rows, room) = (c.data().budgets.db_max_rows, c.data().limit.saturating_sub(c.data().host_used));
    let no_db = || DbErr::Sql("no database is open for this execution".into());
    let (ty, result) = if query {
        let r = c.data().db.as_ref().ok_or_else(no_db).and_then(|db| db.query(&sql, &params, max_rows, room));
        let v = match r {
            Ok(rows) => {
                charge(c, rows.bytes)?;
                let cols = Value::List(rows.columns.into_iter().map(Value::Str).collect());
                let cells = Value::List(rows.rows.into_iter().map(|r| Value::List(r.into_iter().map(sql_value).collect())).collect());
                ok(Value::Record("DbRows".into(), vec![("columns".into(), cols), ("rows".into(), cells)]))
            }
            Err(e) => err(db_error(c, e)?),
        };
        (Type::result(named("DbRows"), named("DbError")), v)
    } else {
        let r = c.data().db.as_ref().ok_or_else(no_db).and_then(|db| db.exec(&sql, &params));
        let v = match r {
            Ok(n) => ok(Value::Int(n)),
            Err(e) => err(db_error(c, e)?),
        };
        (Type::result(Type::Int, named("DbError")), v)
    };
    write_value(c, &ty, &result)
}
```

```rust
/// When an execution's database transaction is committed (spec Phase 3 §3.2): a function call
/// without a runtime error ...
pub fn commit_if_ok(r: &Result<Value, RunError>) -> bool {
    r.is_ok()
}

/// ... and an entry point `fn(List[Str]) -> Int` only with exit code 0.
pub fn commit_if_exit_zero(r: &Result<Value, RunError>) -> bool {
    matches!(r, Ok(Value::Int(0)))
}
```

```rust
/// How an effect is granted on the command line (`--allow <flag>`).
```

```rust
}

/// Opens the database of an execution and starts its transaction.
pub fn open_db(grant: &DbGrant, perms: &Permissions, budgets: Limits, def: &str) -> Result<Db, RunError> {
    let mut db = Db::open(grant, &perms.db_dir(), budgets.db_max_mib).map_err(|m| RunError::new("db_open", def, m).hint("the database file is chosen by the host: `db` is .qal/db/main.sqlite, `db:<name>` is .qal/db/<name>.sqlite, `db:mem` is in memory"))?;
    db.begin().map_err(|e| {
        let m = match e {
            db::DbErr::Busy => format!("the database is busy: another execution held its write lock for more than {} s", db::BUSY_TIMEOUT.as_secs()),
            other => format!("cannot start the transaction: {other:?}"),
        };
        RunError::new("timeout", def, m)
    })?;
    Ok(db)
}

/// Ends the transaction of an execution by `commit(result)`; a failed commit turns the result into
/// an error. Returns `commit`, `rollback` or `none`.
pub fn finish_db(db: Option<Db>, result: &mut Result<Value, RunError>, commit: &dyn Fn(&Result<Value, RunError>) -> bool, def: &str) -> &'static str {
    let Some(mut db) = db else { return "none" };
    match db.finish(commit(result)) {
        Ok(what) => what,
        Err(m) => {
            if result.is_ok() {
                *result = Err(RunError::new("trap", def, m));
            }
            "rollback"
        }
    }
```

```rust
}

/// Decodes a value the host acts on (an `HttpCall`, SQL parameters, a handler's `HttpResponse`):
/// exactly, without cutting strings, and failing on anything malformed or on more than `max_bytes`
/// (string bytes plus 8 per node).
pub fn decode_exact(tp: &TProgram, mem: &[u8], ty: &Type, slot: i64, max_bytes: usize) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let mut used = 0usize;
    exact_at(tp, mem, ty, slot, 0, max_bytes, &mut used)
}

fn exact_at(tp: &TProgram, mem: &[u8], ty: &Type, slot: i64, depth: usize, max: usize, used: &mut usize) -> Result<Value, String> {
    *used = used.saturating_add(8);
    if *used > max {
        return Err(format!("the value is larger than {max} bytes"));
    }
    if depth > MAX_DEPTH {
        return Err("the value is nested too deeply".into());
    }
    let ptr = slot as u32 as usize;
    let bad = || format!("invalid {ty} at {ptr}");
    Ok(match ty {
        Type::Int => Value::Int(slot),
        Type::Float => Value::Float(f64::from_bits(slot as u64)),
        Type::Bool => Value::Bool(slot != 0),
        Type::Unit => Value::Unit,
        Type::Str => {
            let len = u32_at(mem, ptr).ok_or_else(bad)? as usize;
            *used = used.saturating_add(len);
            if *used > max {
                return Err(format!("the value is larger than {max} bytes"));
            }
            let bytes = mem.get(ptr + 4..ptr + 4 + len).ok_or_else(bad)?;
            Value::Str(String::from_utf8(bytes.to_vec()).map_err(|_| format!("a string is not valid UTF-8 at {ptr}"))?)
        }
        Type::List(elem) => {
            let len = u32_at(mem, ptr).ok_or_else(bad)? as usize;
            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(len.min(1024));
            for k in 0..len {
                let s = slot_at(mem, ptr + 8 + 8 * k).ok_or_else(bad)?;
                out.push(exact_at(tp, mem, elem, s, depth + 1, max, used)?);
            }
            Value::List(out)
        }
        Type::Named(name) if tp.is_record(ty) => {
            let mut fields = Vec::new();
            for (k, (f, ft)) in tp.record_fields(ty).iter().enumerate() {
                let s = slot_at(mem, ptr + 8 * k).ok_or_else(bad)?;
                fields.push((f.clone(), exact_at(tp, mem, ft, s, depth + 1, max, used)?));
            }
            Value::Record(name.clone(), fields)
        }
        Type::Named(_) | Type::Option(_) | Type::Result(..) => {
            let tag = u32_at(mem, ptr).ok_or_else(bad)?;
            let names = tp.variant_names(ty);
            let name = names.get(tag as usize).ok_or_else(bad)?;
            let mut payload = Vec::new();
            for (k, ft) in tp.variant_fields(ty, tag).iter().enumerate() {
                let s = slot_at(mem, ptr + 8 + 8 * k).ok_or_else(bad)?;
                payload.push(exact_at(tp, mem, ft, s, depth + 1, max, used)?);
            }
            Value::Variant(name.clone(), payload)
        }
        Type::Fn(..) | Type::Var(_) => return Err(format!("{ty} cannot be read by the host")),
    })
```

```rust
/// A local HTTP server answering `n` connections: `respond(request text)` gives the raw response.
fn serve_http(n: usize, respond: fn(&str) -> Vec<u8>) -> u16 {
    let l = std::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let port = l.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        use std::io::{Read, Write};
        for s in l.incoming().take(n) {
            let mut s = s.unwrap();
            s.set_read_timeout(Some(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
            let mut buf = Vec::new();
            let mut chunk = [0u8; 4096];
            // Read the head, then as much body as `content-length` says.
            loop {
                let k = s.read(&mut chunk).unwrap_or(0);
                buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..k]);
                let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf).to_string();
                if let Some(end) = text.find("\r\n\r\n") {
                    let len = text.lines().find_map(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().strip_prefix("content-length:").map(|v| v.trim().parse::<usize>().unwrap_or(0))).unwrap_or(0);
                    if buf.len() >= end + 4 + len || k == 0 {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if k == 0 {
                    break;
                }
            }
            let _ = s.write_all(&respond(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf)));
        }
    });
    port
}

const FETCH: &str = "fn fetch(m: Str, u: Str, b: Str) -> Str !net = match http(HttpCall { method: m, url: u, headers: [HttpHeader { name: \"x-test\", value: \"1\" }], body: b }) {\n\
      Ok(r) => from_int(r.status) ++ \"|\" ++ join(map(r.headers, show), \";\") ++ \"|\" ++ r.body,\n\
      Err(NetDenied(m)) => \"denied: \" ++ m, Err(NetInvalid(m)) => \"invalid: \" ++ m, Err(NetConnect(m)) => \"connect: \" ++ m, Err(NetTimeout) => \"timeout\", Err(NetTooLarge) => \"too large\" }\n\
    fn show(h: HttpHeader) -> Str = h.name ++ \"=\" ++ h.value\n\
    fn twice(u: Str) -> Str !net = fetch(\"GET\", u, \"\") ++ fetch(\"GET\", u, \"\")";

fn net_runner(allow: &str) -> Runner {
    Runner::new(compile(FETCH, Entry::All)).unwrap().with_permissions(Permissions::parse(allow).unwrap())
}

fn fetch(r: &Runner, m: &str, u: &str, b: &str) -> String {
    match r.call("fetch", &[Value::Str(m.into()), Value::Str(u.into()), Value::Str(b.into())]) {
        Ok(Value::Str(s)) => s,
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
}

#[test]
fn http_calls_reach_granted_hosts_only() {
    let port = serve_http(2, |req| {
        let first = req.lines().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
        let ua = req.lines().find(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("user-agent:")).unwrap_or("").to_string();
        let body = format!("{first}\n{}\n{}", ua.to_ascii_lowercase(), req.split("\r\n\r\n").nth(1).unwrap_or(""));
        format!("HTTP/1.1 201 Created\r\nX-Reply: Yes\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len()).into_bytes()
    });
    let r = net_runner(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{port}"));
    let out = fetch(&r, "POST", &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{port}/todos?x=1"), "{\"a\":1}");
    let (status, rest) = out.split_once('|').unwrap();
    assert_eq!(status, "201");
    assert!(rest.contains("x-reply=Yes"), "header names are lowercase: {rest}");
    assert!(rest.contains("POST /todos?x=1 HTTP/1.1"), "{rest}");
    assert!(rest.contains(&format!("user-agent: qal/{}", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"))), "{rest}");
    assert!(rest.ends_with("{\"a\":1}"), "{rest}");
    // Another port, another host, an IP spelled as a name: not granted, and no connection is made.
    assert!(fetch(&r, "GET", &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}/", port + 1), "").starts_with(&format!("denied: `127.0.0.1:{}` is not granted", port + 1)));
    assert!(fetch(&r, "GET", "https://example.com/", "").contains("it needs `net:example.com`"));
    assert!(fetch(&r, "GET", &format!("ftp://127.0.0.1:{port}/"), "").starts_with("invalid: unsupported URL scheme"));
    // Without the `net` grant the function cannot run at all.
    let e = net_runner("io").call("fetch", &[Value::Str("GET".into()), Value::Str("http://x/".into()), Value::Str(String::new())]).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, e.hints.clone()), ("permission_denied", vec!["grant them with `--allow net:<host>`".to_string()]));
    assert!(Permissions::parse("net").unwrap_err().contains("needs a host"));
}

#[test]
fn redirects_are_returned_not_followed() {
    let target = serve_http(1, |_| b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 6\r\n\r\nSECRET".to_vec());
    let port = serve_http(1, |_| b"HTTP/1.1 302 Found\r\nLocation: http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/\r\nContent-Length: 0\r\n\r\n".to_vec());
    let r = net_runner(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{port},net:127.0.0.1:{target}"));
    let out = fetch(&r, "GET", &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{port}/"), "");
    assert!(out.starts_with("302|") && out.contains("location=http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/"), "{out}");
    assert!(!out.contains("SECRET"));
}

#[test]
fn responses_must_be_utf8_and_bounded() {
    let big = serve_http(1, |_| {
        let n = 5 << 20;
        let mut v = format!("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: {n}\r\n\r\n").into_bytes();
        v.extend(std::iter::repeat_n(b'x', n));
        v
    });
    assert_eq!(fetch(&net_runner(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{big}")), "GET", &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{big}/"), ""), "too large");
    let bad = serve_http(1, |_| b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n\xff\xfe".to_vec());
    assert_eq!(fetch(&net_runner(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{bad}")), "GET", &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{bad}/"), ""), "invalid: response body is not UTF-8");
    let closed = std::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap().local_addr().unwrap().port();
    assert!(fetch(&net_runner(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{closed}")), "GET", &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{closed}/"), "").starts_with("connect: "));
    // Response bodies count against the memory limit.
    let mid = serve_http(1, |_| {
        let n = 3 << 20;
        let mut v = format!("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: {n}\r\n\r\n").into_bytes();
        v.extend(std::iter::repeat_n(b'y', n));
        v
    });
    let r = net_runner(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{mid}")).with_memory_limit(2 << 20);
    let e = r.call("fetch", &[Value::Str("GET".into()), Value::Str(format!("http://127.0.0.1:{mid}/")), Value::Str(String::new())]).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, serde_json::to_value(&e.values).unwrap()["host"].clone()), ("out_of_memory", json!(true)));
}

#[test]
fn outgoing_calls_have_a_budget() {
    let port = serve_http(1, |_| b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\nok".to_vec());
    let r = net_runner(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{port}")).with_limits(Limits { net_calls: 1, ..Limits::default() });
    let e = r.call("twice", &[Value::Str(format!("http://127.0.0.1:{port}/"))]).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, serde_json::to_value(&e.values).unwrap()), ("budget_exceeded", json!({"budget": 1, "used": 2, "kind": "net_calls"})));
}
```

```rust
}

fn header_list(headers: Vec<(String, String)>) -> Value {
    Value::List(headers.into_iter().map(|(n, v)| Value::Record("HttpHeader".into(), vec![("name".into(), Value::Str(n)), ("value".into(), Value::Str(v))])).collect())
}

/// The fields of a decoded `HttpHeader` list.
pub fn headers_of(v: &Value) -> Option<Vec<(String, String)>> {
    let Value::List(xs) = v else { return None };
    xs.iter()
        .map(|h| match h {
            Value::Record(_, f) => match f.as_slice() {
                [(_, Value::Str(n)), (_, Value::Str(v))] => Some((n.clone(), v.clone())),
                _ => None,
            },
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect()
}

fn field<'v>(fields: &'v [(String, Value)], name: &str) -> Option<&'v Value> {
    fields.iter().find(|(n, _)| n == name).map(|(_, v)| v)
}

fn call_of(v: &Value) -> Option<Call> {
    let Value::Record(_, f) = v else { return None };
    let s = |n: &str| match field(f, n) {
        Some(Value::Str(s)) => Some(s.clone()),
        _ => None,
    };
    Some(Call { method: s("method")?, url: s("url")?, headers: headers_of(field(f, "headers")?)?, body: s("body")? })
}

/// Runs `http` for the guest: budget, grants and SSRF checks, the request, and the `Result`.
fn net_call(c: &mut Caller<'_, HostState>, ptr: i32) -> Result<i32, wasmtime::Error> {
    let budget = c.data().budgets.net_calls;
    let calls = c.data().net_calls + 1;
    if calls > budget {
        return Err(budget_exceeded(c, "net_calls", budget, calls));
    }
    c.data_mut().net_calls = calls;
    let v = read_value(c, &named("HttpCall"), ptr)?;
    let call = call_of(&v).ok_or_else(|| wasmtime::Error::msg("internal error: malformed HttpCall"))?;
    let left = c.data().deadline.map_or(net::TOTAL_TIMEOUT, |d| d.saturating_duration_since(std::time::Instant::now()));
    let lookup = c.data().lookup;
    let result = net::plan(&c.data().perms.net, &call, lookup, left.min(net::CONNECT_TIMEOUT)).and_then(|(url, addrs)| net::send(&call, url, addrs, left));
    let v = match result {
        Ok(reply) => {
            charge(c, reply.body.len() + reply.headers.iter().map(|(n, v)| n.len() + v.len() + 16).sum::<usize>())?;
            ok(Value::Record("HttpResponse".into(), vec![("status".into(), Value::Int(reply.status)), ("headers".into(), header_list(reply.headers)), ("body".into(), Value::Str(reply.body))]))
        }
        Err(e) => err(match e {
            NetErr::Denied(m) => variant("NetDenied", vec![Value::Str(m)]),
            NetErr::Invalid(m) => variant("NetInvalid", vec![Value::Str(m)]),
            NetErr::Connect(m) => variant("NetConnect", vec![Value::Str(m)]),
            NetErr::Timeout => variant("NetTimeout", vec![]),
            NetErr::TooLarge => variant("NetTooLarge", vec![]),
        }),
    };
    write_value(c, &Type::result(named("HttpResponse"), named("NetError")), &v)
```
