Create `qal/crates/qal-run/src/net.rs`:

```rust
//! The `net` effect: outgoing HTTP requests through the host (Phase 3 spec §3.1, §5.1).
//!
//! A request goes out only to a granted host and port (`net:<host>[:port]`). DNS names are
//! resolved by the host, every address is checked against the private and reserved ranges (one
//! blocked address blocks the name), and the connection uses exactly the checked addresses (no
//! second lookup, so DNS rebinding between check and connect is impossible). An IP literal grant
//! allows exactly that address and port. No redirects are followed, proxies are never used, and
//! connect time, total time, response size and the number of requests are bounded.

use std::io::Read;
use std::net::{IpAddr, Ipv4Addr, Ipv6Addr, SocketAddr, ToSocketAddrs};
use std::sync::mpsc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// The host of a grant.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum NetHost {
    /// A DNS name, lowercase ASCII.
    Name(String),
    /// `*.<suffix>`: every name below `<suffix>` (not `<suffix>` itself).
    Below(String),
    /// An IP literal: exactly this address.
    Ip(IpAddr),
}

/// A `net:<host>[:port]` grant.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct NetGrant {
    pub host: NetHost,
    /// None: only the default ports (80 for http, 443 for https).
    pub port: Option<u16>,
}

/// Connect timeout of one request.
pub const CONNECT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);
/// Total time of one request (never more than the execution has left).
pub const TOTAL_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);
/// Largest accepted response body.
pub const MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES: usize = 4 << 20;
/// Headers the host sets itself; the guest may not.
const HOST_HEADERS: [&str; 5] = ["host", "content-length", "connection", "user-agent", "transfer-encoding"];

fn valid_name(n: &str) -> bool {
    let labels: Vec<&str> = n.split('.').collect();
    n.len() <= 253
        && labels.iter().all(|l| !l.is_empty() && l.len() <= 63 && !l.starts_with('-') && !l.ends_with('-') && l.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '-'))
        // A numeric last label makes the URL parser read the name as an IPv4 address.
        && labels.last().is_some_and(|l| !l.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) && !l.starts_with("0x"))
}

fn is_localhost(n: &str) -> bool {
    n == "localhost" || n.ends_with(".localhost")
}

impl NetGrant {
    /// Parses `net:<host>`, `net:<host>:<port>`, `net:*.<domain>[:port]`, `net:1.2.3.4[:port]`,
    /// `net:[::1][:port]`.
    pub fn parse(item: &str) -> Result<NetGrant, String> {
        let spec = item.strip_prefix("net:").unwrap_or("");
        if spec.is_empty() {
            return Err("`net` needs a host: `net:<host>[:port]`".into());
        }
        let (host, port) = if let Some(rest) = spec.strip_prefix('[') {
            let (ip, after) = rest.split_once(']').ok_or_else(|| format!("`{item}`: missing `]`"))?;
            let port = match after {
                "" => None,
                p => Some(p.strip_prefix(':').ok_or_else(|| format!("`{item}`: expected `:<port>` after `]`"))?),
            };
            let ip: Ipv6Addr = ip.parse().map_err(|_| format!("`{item}`: not an IPv6 address"))?;
            (NetHost::Ip(IpAddr::V6(ip)), port)
        } else {
            let (h, p) = match spec.rsplit_once(':') {
                Some((h, p)) => (h, Some(p)),
                None => (spec, None),
            };
            let host = if let Ok(ip) = h.parse::<Ipv4Addr>() {
                NetHost::Ip(IpAddr::V4(ip))
            } else if let Some(suffix) = h.strip_prefix("*.") {
                if !valid_name(suffix) || !suffix.contains('.') {
                    return Err(format!("`{item}`: `*.` must be followed by a domain with at least two labels, e.g. `net:*.example.com`"));
                }
                NetHost::Below(suffix.to_string())
            } else if valid_name(h) {
                NetHost::Name(h.to_string())
            } else {
                return Err(format!("`{item}`: `{h}` is not a lowercase DNS name (IDNA names in their `xn--` form) or an IP address"));
            };
            (host, p)
        };
        if let NetHost::Name(n) | NetHost::Below(n) = &host {
            if is_localhost(n) {
                return Err(format!("`{item}`: `localhost` names are not allowed; grant the address, e.g. `net:127.0.0.1:{}`", port.unwrap_or("8080")));
            }
        }
        let port = match port {
            None => None,
            Some(p) => Some(p.parse::<u16>().ok().filter(|p| *p > 0).ok_or_else(|| format!("`{item}`: `{p}` is not a port (1-65535)"))?),
        };
        Ok(NetGrant { host, port })
    }

    /// The grant as written.
    pub fn spec(&self) -> String {
        let host = match &self.host {
            NetHost::Name(n) => n.clone(),
            NetHost::Below(n) => format!("*.{n}"),
            NetHost::Ip(IpAddr::V4(ip)) => ip.to_string(),
            NetHost::Ip(IpAddr::V6(ip)) => format!("[{ip}]"),
        };
        match self.port {
            Some(p) => format!("net:{host}:{p}"),
            None => format!("net:{host}"),
        }
    }

    fn allows_port(&self, port: u16, default: u16) -> bool {
        match self.port {
            Some(p) => p == port,
            None => port == default && (port == 80 || port == 443),
        }
    }
}

/// An IPv4 address embedded in an IPv6 one (mapped, compatible, NAT64, 6to4), which reaches the
/// same IPv4 host.
fn embedded_v4(ip: &Ipv6Addr) -> Option<Ipv4Addr> {
    let s = ip.segments();
    let v4 = |a: u16, b: u16| Ipv4Addr::new((a >> 8) as u8, a as u8, (b >> 8) as u8, b as u8);
    if let Some(v) = ip.to_ipv4_mapped() {
        return Some(v);
    }
    match s {
        // ::a.b.c.d (IPv4-compatible; :: and ::1 themselves are handled as IPv6)
        [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, a, b] if (a, b) != (0, 0) && (a, b) != (0, 1) => Some(v4(a, b)),
        // 64:ff9b::/96 and 64:ff9b:1::/48 (NAT64)
        [0x64, 0xff9b, 0, 0, 0, 0, a, b] => Some(v4(a, b)),
        [0x64, 0xff9b, 1, ..] => Some(v4(s[6], s[7])),
        // 2002:AABB:CCDD::/48 (6to4)
        [0x2002, a, b, ..] => Some(v4(a, b)),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn blocked_v4(ip: Ipv4Addr) -> bool {
    let [a, b, c, _] = ip.octets();
    a == 0 || a == 10 || a == 127 || (a == 100 && (64..128).contains(&b)) || (a == 169 && b == 254) || (a == 172 && (16..32).contains(&b))
        || (a == 192 && b == 0 && c == 0)
        || (a == 192 && b == 168)
        || (a == 198 && (b == 18 || b == 19))
        || a >= 224
}

/// True if a DNS name resolving to `ip` must not be contacted: private, loopback, link-local
/// (cloud metadata), shared, benchmark, multicast and reserved ranges, also when embedded in IPv6.
pub fn blocked(ip: IpAddr) -> bool {
    match ip {
        IpAddr::V4(v4) => blocked_v4(v4),
        IpAddr::V6(v6) => {
            if let Some(v4) = embedded_v4(&v6) {
                return blocked_v4(v4);
            }
            let s0 = v6.segments()[0];
            v6.is_unspecified() || v6.is_loopback() || (s0 & 0xfe00) == 0xfc00 || (s0 & 0xffc0) == 0xfe80 || (s0 & 0xff00) == 0xff00
        }
    }
}

/// The same address for comparing with an IP grant (IPv4-mapped IPv6 is its IPv4 address).
fn canonical_ip(ip: IpAddr) -> IpAddr {
    match ip {
        IpAddr::V6(v6) => v6.to_ipv4_mapped().map(IpAddr::V4).unwrap_or(ip),
        v4 => v4,
    }
}

/// A request from the guest (`HttpCall`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Call {
    pub method: String,
    pub url: String,
    pub headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
    pub body: String,
}

/// A response (`HttpResponse`): status, headers with lowercase names, UTF-8 body.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Reply {
    pub status: i64,
    pub headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
    pub body: String,
}

/// A failed request (`NetError`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum NetErr {
    Denied(String),
    Invalid(String),
    Connect(String),
    Timeout,
    TooLarge,
}

/// Looks up a name (in a helper thread, so a slow resolver cannot outlast `limit`).
pub type Lookup = fn(&str, u16, Duration) -> std::io::Result<Vec<SocketAddr>>;

/// The system resolver, bounded by `limit`.
pub fn system_lookup(host: &str, port: u16, limit: Duration) -> std::io::Result<Vec<SocketAddr>> {
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
    let name = host.to_string();
    std::thread::Builder::new().name("qal-dns".into()).spawn(move || {
        let _ = tx.send((name.as_str(), port).to_socket_addrs().map(|a| a.collect::<Vec<_>>()));
    })?;
    rx.recv_timeout(limit).map_err(|_| std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "DNS lookup timed out"))?
}

pub fn is_token(s: &str) -> bool {
    !s.is_empty() && s.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~".contains(&b))
}

/// Checks the call against the grants and resolves its destination: the URL and the addresses to
/// connect to.
pub fn plan(grants: &[NetGrant], call: &Call, lookup: Lookup, limit: Duration) -> Result<(url::Url, Vec<SocketAddr>), NetErr> {
    if !is_token(&call.method) {
        return Err(NetErr::Invalid(format!("`{}` is not an HTTP method", call.method.chars().take(40).collect::<String>())));
    }
    for (n, v) in &call.headers {
        if !is_token(n) {
            return Err(NetErr::Invalid(format!("`{}` is not a header name", n.chars().take(40).collect::<String>())));
        }
        if v.bytes().any(|b| b == b'\r' || b == b'\n' || b == 0) {
            return Err(NetErr::Invalid(format!("the value of header `{n}` contains CR, LF or NUL")));
        }
        if HOST_HEADERS.iter().any(|h| h.eq_ignore_ascii_case(n)) {
            return Err(NetErr::Invalid(format!("header `{n}` is set by the host")));
        }
    }
    let url = url::Url::parse(&call.url).map_err(|e| NetErr::Invalid(format!("invalid URL: {e}")))?;
    let default = match url.scheme() {
        "http" => 80,
        "https" => 443,
        s => return Err(NetErr::Invalid(format!("unsupported URL scheme `{s}` (only http and https)"))),
    };
    if !url.username().is_empty() || url.password().is_some() {
        return Err(NetErr::Invalid("URLs with user information (`user:pass@`) are not allowed".into()));
    }
    let port = url.port_or_known_default().unwrap_or(default);
    let host = url.host().ok_or_else(|| NetErr::Invalid("the URL has no host".into()))?;
    let shown = match &host {
        url::Host::Domain(d) => d.to_string(),
        url::Host::Ipv4(ip) => ip.to_string(),
        url::Host::Ipv6(ip) => format!("[{ip}]"),
    };
    let denied = || {
        let need = match &host {
            url::Host::Domain(d) => format!("net:{}", d.trim_end_matches('.')),
            _ => format!("net:{shown}"),
        };
        let need = if port == default { need } else { format!("{need}:{port}") };
        NetErr::Denied(format!("`{shown}:{port}` is not granted; it needs `{need}`"))
    };
    match host {
        url::Host::Ipv4(_) | url::Host::Ipv6(_) => {
            let ip = canonical_ip(match host {
                url::Host::Ipv4(v4) => IpAddr::V4(v4),
                url::Host::Ipv6(v6) => IpAddr::V6(v6),
                url::Host::Domain(_) => return Err(denied()),
            });
            let ok = grants.iter().any(|g| matches!(g.host, NetHost::Ip(gip) if canonical_ip(gip) == ip) && g.allows_port(port, default));
            if !ok {
                return Err(denied());
            }
            Ok((url, vec![SocketAddr::new(ip, port)]))
        }
        url::Host::Domain(d) => {
            let name = d.trim_end_matches('.').to_ascii_lowercase();
            let ok = grants.iter().any(|g| {
                let host_ok = match &g.host {
                    NetHost::Name(n) => *n == name,
                    NetHost::Below(s) => name.len() > s.len() + 1 && name.ends_with(s.as_str()) && name.as_bytes()[name.len() - s.len() - 1] == b'.',
                    NetHost::Ip(_) => false,
                };
                host_ok && g.allows_port(port, default)
            });
            if !ok || is_localhost(&name) {
                return Err(denied());
            }
            let addrs = lookup(&name, port, limit).map_err(|e| if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::TimedOut { NetErr::Timeout } else { NetErr::Connect(format!("cannot resolve `{name}`: {e}")) })?;
            if addrs.is_empty() {
                return Err(NetErr::Connect(format!("`{name}` has no address")));
            }
            if let Some(bad) = addrs.iter().find(|a| blocked(a.ip())) {
                return Err(NetErr::Denied(format!("`{name}` resolves to the private or reserved address {}; names may only reach public addresses (grant an IP literal for local services)", bad.ip())));
            }
            Ok((url, addrs.into_iter().map(|a| SocketAddr::new(a.ip(), port)).collect()))
        }
    }
}

/// Sends the request to the planned addresses and reads the response within `limit`.
pub fn send(call: &Call, url: url::Url, addrs: Vec<SocketAddr>, limit: Duration) -> Result<Reply, NetErr> {
    let start = Instant::now();
    let total = limit.min(TOTAL_TIMEOUT);
    let fixed = addrs.clone();
    let agent = ureq::AgentBuilder::new()
        .redirects(0)
        .timeout_connect(CONNECT_TIMEOUT.min(total))
        .timeout(total)
        .max_idle_connections(0)
        .user_agent(concat!("qal/", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")))
        .resolver(move |_: &str| -> std::io::Result<Vec<SocketAddr>> { Ok(fixed.clone()) })
        .build();
    let mut req = agent.request_url(&call.method, &url).set("connection", "close");
    for (n, v) in &call.headers {
        req = req.set(n, v);
    }
    let resp = match req.send_bytes(call.body.as_bytes()) {
        Ok(r) | Err(ureq::Error::Status(_, r)) => r,
        Err(ureq::Error::Transport(t)) => {
            let msg = t.to_string();
            let timed_out = start.elapsed() >= total || msg.contains("timed out") || msg.contains("Timeout") || msg.contains("WouldBlock");
            return Err(match t.kind() {
                _ if timed_out => NetErr::Timeout,
                ureq::ErrorKind::InvalidUrl | ureq::ErrorKind::UnknownScheme => NetErr::Invalid(msg),
                _ => NetErr::Connect(msg),
            });
        }
    };
    let status = resp.status() as i64;
    let mut headers = Vec::new();
    for name in resp.headers_names() {
        for v in resp.all(&name) {
            headers.push((name.to_ascii_lowercase(), v.trim().to_string()));
        }
    }
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    let read = resp.into_reader().take(MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut bytes);
    if let Err(e) = read {
        return Err(if start.elapsed() >= total || e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::TimedOut || e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::WouldBlock { NetErr::Timeout } else { NetErr::Connect(format!("reading the response: {e}")) });
    }
    if bytes.len() > MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES {
        return Err(NetErr::TooLarge);
    }
    let body = String::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| NetErr::Invalid("response body is not UTF-8".into()))?;
    Ok(Reply { status, headers, body })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn g(s: &str) -> NetGrant {
        NetGrant::parse(s).unwrap()
    }

    fn call(url: &str) -> Call {
        Call { method: "GET".into(), url: url.into(), headers: vec![], body: String::new() }
    }

    fn fake(host: &str, port: u16, _: Duration) -> std::io::Result<Vec<SocketAddr>> {
        let ips: Vec<IpAddr> = match host {
            "public.example.com" | "api.example.com" | "a.b.example.com" => vec!["93.184.216.34".parse().unwrap()],
            "rebind.example.com" => vec!["93.184.216.34".parse().unwrap(), "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap()],
            "meta.example.com" => vec!["169.254.169.254".parse().unwrap()],
            "mapped.example.com" => vec!["::ffff:10.0.0.1".parse().unwrap()],
            "v6.example.com" => vec!["2606:2800:220:1:248:1893:25c8:1946".parse().unwrap()],
            "slow.example.com" => return Err(std::io::Error::new(std::io::ErrorKind::TimedOut, "slow")),
            _ => return Err(std::io::Error::other("no such host")),
        };
        Ok(ips.into_iter().map(|ip| SocketAddr::new(ip, port)).collect())
    }

    fn plan_with(grants: &[&str], url: &str) -> Result<Vec<SocketAddr>, NetErr> {
        let grants: Vec<NetGrant> = grants.iter().map(|s| g(s)).collect();
        plan(&grants, &call(url), fake, Duration::from_secs(1)).map(|(_, a)| a)
    }

    #[test]
    fn grants_parse() {
        assert_eq!(g("net:api.example.com"), NetGrant { host: NetHost::Name("api.example.com".into()), port: None });
        assert_eq!(g("net:api.example.com:8443").port, Some(8443));
        assert_eq!(g("net:*.example.com").host, NetHost::Below("example.com".into()));
        assert_eq!(g("net:127.0.0.1:9000"), NetGrant { host: NetHost::Ip("127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap()), port: Some(9000) });
        assert_eq!(g("net:[::1]:9000").host, NetHost::Ip("::1".parse().unwrap()));
        assert_eq!(g("net:[::1]").port, None);
        for s in ["net:[::1]:9000", "net:*.example.com:81", "net:10.1.2.3", "net:xn--bcher-kva.example"] {
            assert_eq!(g(s).spec(), s);
        }
        let e = |s: &str| NetGrant::parse(s).unwrap_err();
        assert!(e("net:").contains("needs a host"));
        assert!(e("net:localhost:8080").contains("`localhost` names are not allowed"));
        assert!(e("net:*.localhost").contains("localhost"));
        assert!(e("net:API.example.com").contains("not a lowercase DNS name"));
        assert!(e("net:2130706433").contains("not a lowercase DNS name"), "decimal IPv4 is not a name");
        assert!(e("net:0x7f.0.0.1").contains("not a lowercase DNS name"));
        assert!(e("net:010.0.0.1").contains("not a lowercase DNS name"), "octal-looking octets are refused");
        assert!(e("net:*.com").contains("at least two labels"));
        assert!(e("net:a.com:0").contains("not a port"));
        assert!(e("net:a.com:99999").contains("not a port"));
        assert!(e("net:[::1").contains("missing `]`"));
        assert!(e("net:bad_name.com").contains("not a lowercase"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn private_and_reserved_ranges_are_blocked_for_names() {
        for ip in ["0.0.0.0", "10.1.2.3", "100.64.0.1", "127.0.0.1", "169.254.169.254", "172.16.0.1", "172.31.255.255", "192.0.0.8", "192.168.1.1", "198.18.0.1", "224.0.0.1", "240.0.0.1", "255.255.255.255", "::", "::1", "fc00::1", "fd12::1", "fe80::1", "ff02::1", "::ffff:127.0.0.1", "::ffff:10.0.0.1", "64:ff9b::a9fe:a9fe", "::7f00:1", "2002:a9fe:a9fe::1", "64:ff9b:1::7f00:1"] {
            assert!(blocked(ip.parse().unwrap()), "{ip}");
        }
        for ip in ["93.184.216.34", "8.8.8.8", "172.32.0.1", "100.128.0.1", "2606:4700::1111", "::ffff:8.8.8.8", "2002:808:808::1"] {
            assert!(!blocked(ip.parse().unwrap()), "{ip}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn destinations_must_be_granted_and_public() {
        assert_eq!(plan_with(&["net:api.example.com"], "https://api.example.com/v1?x=1").unwrap(), vec!["93.184.216.34:443".parse().unwrap()]);
        assert!(plan_with(&["net:api.example.com"], "http://api.example.com/").is_ok(), "port 80 for http");
        assert!(matches!(plan_with(&["net:api.example.com"], "http://api.example.com:8080/"), Err(NetErr::Denied(m)) if m.contains("net:api.example.com:8080")));
        assert!(plan_with(&["net:api.example.com:8080"], "http://api.example.com:8080/").is_ok());
        assert!(matches!(plan_with(&["net:api.example.com:8080"], "http://api.example.com/"), Err(NetErr::Denied(_))));
        assert!(plan_with(&["net:*.example.com"], "https://a.b.example.com/").is_ok());
        assert!(matches!(plan_with(&["net:*.example.com"], "https://example.com/"), Err(NetErr::Denied(_))), "not the domain itself");
        assert!(matches!(plan_with(&["net:*.example.com"], "https://evilexample.com/"), Err(NetErr::Denied(_))));
        assert!(plan_with(&["net:api.example.com"], "https://API.Example.COM./").is_ok(), "the URL parser lowercases; a trailing dot is the same name");
        // DNS answers with any private address are refused (rebinding, cloud metadata, mapped IPv6).
        for host in ["rebind", "meta", "mapped"] {
            let url = format!("https://{host}.example.com/");
            assert!(matches!(plan_with(&[&format!("net:{host}.example.com")], &url), Err(NetErr::Denied(m)) if m.contains("private or reserved")), "{host}");
        }
        assert!(plan_with(&["net:v6.example.com"], "https://v6.example.com/").is_ok());
        assert_eq!(plan_with(&["net:slow.example.com"], "https://slow.example.com/"), Err(NetErr::Timeout));
        assert!(matches!(plan_with(&["net:nowhere.example.com"], "https://nowhere.example.com/"), Err(NetErr::Connect(_))));
        // IP literals need an IP grant; every spelling of an address is that address.
        assert_eq!(plan_with(&["net:127.0.0.1:9000"], "http://127.0.0.1:9000/").unwrap(), vec!["127.0.0.1:9000".parse().unwrap()]);
        for url in ["http://2130706433:9000/", "http://0x7f000001:9000/", "http://017700000001:9000/", "http://127.1:9000/", "http://[::ffff:127.0.0.1]:9000/"] {
            assert_eq!(plan_with(&["net:127.0.0.1:9000"], url).unwrap(), vec!["127.0.0.1:9000".parse().unwrap()], "{url}");
            assert!(matches!(plan_with(&["net:api.example.com"], url), Err(NetErr::Denied(_))), "{url}");
        }
        assert!(matches!(plan_with(&["net:127.0.0.1:9000"], "http://127.0.0.1:9001/"), Err(NetErr::Denied(_))));
        assert!(plan_with(&["net:[::1]:9000"], "http://[::1]:9000/").is_ok());
        assert!(matches!(plan_with(&[], "http://localhost/"), Err(NetErr::Denied(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn malformed_calls_are_invalid() {
        let grants = vec![g("net:api.example.com")];
        let inv = |c: Call| match plan(&grants, &c, fake, Duration::from_secs(1)) {
            Err(NetErr::Invalid(m)) => m,
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        };
        assert!(inv(call("ftp://api.example.com/")).contains("unsupported URL scheme"));
        assert!(inv(call("file:///etc/passwd")).contains("unsupported URL scheme"));
        assert!(inv(call("https://user:pw@api.example.com/")).contains("user information"));
        assert!(inv(call("https://user@api.example.com/")).contains("user information"));
        assert!(inv(call("not a url")).contains("invalid URL"));
        assert!(inv(Call { method: "GE T".into(), ..call("https://api.example.com/") }).contains("not an HTTP method"));
        for h in ["host", "Content-Length", "connection", "User-Agent", "transfer-encoding"] {
            assert!(inv(Call { headers: vec![(h.into(), "x".into())], ..call("https://api.example.com/") }).contains("set by the host"), "{h}");
        }
        assert!(inv(Call { headers: vec![("x-a".into(), "v\r\nInjected: 1".into())], ..call("https://api.example.com/") }).contains("CR, LF or NUL"));
        assert!(inv(Call { headers: vec![("bad name".into(), "v".into())], ..call("https://api.example.com/") }).contains("not a header name"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_silent_server_times_out_and_tls_is_required_for_https() {
        let l = std::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
        let addr = l.local_addr().unwrap();
        let hold = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let held: Vec<_> = l.incoming().take(2).collect();
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2));
            drop(held);
        });
        let grants = vec![g(&format!("net:127.0.0.1:{}", addr.port()))];
        let t = Instant::now();
        let c = call(&format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}/", addr.port()));
        let (url, addrs) = plan(&grants, &c, fake, Duration::from_secs(1)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(send(&c, url, addrs, Duration::from_millis(300)), Err(NetErr::Timeout));
        assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(2), "{:?}", t.elapsed());
        // https to a server that does not speak TLS fails (certificates are always verified).
        let c = call(&format!("https://127.0.0.1:{}/", addr.port()));
        let (url, addrs) = plan(&grants, &c, fake, Duration::from_secs(1)).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(send(&c, url, addrs, Duration::from_millis(300)), Err(NetErr::Timeout | NetErr::Connect(_))));
        hold.join().unwrap();
    }
}
```

```rust
/// Runs `f` on a thread with a guest-sized stack (like a server worker).
fn on_guest_thread<T: Send>(f: impl FnOnce() -> T + Send) -> T {
    std::thread::scope(|s| std::thread::Builder::new().stack_size(GUEST_THREAD_STACK).spawn_scoped(s, f).unwrap().join().unwrap())
}

const PREPARED: &str = "fn hog(mib: Int) -> Int = len(grow(\"0123456789abcdef\", mib * 1048576))\n\
    fn grow(s: Str, n: Int) -> Str = if len(s) >= n then s else grow(s ++ s, n)\n\
    fn spin(n: Int) -> Int = spin(n + 1)\n\
    fn quick(n: Int) -> Int = n + 1\n\
    fn text(n: Int) -> Str = slice(grow(\"ab\", n), 0, n)\n\
    fn rows() -> Int !db = match db_query(\"SELECT 1\", []) { Ok(r) => len(r.rows), Err(_) => 0 }";

fn prepared(perms: Permissions, memory: usize) -> Runner {
    Runner::prepared(compile(PREPARED, Entry::All), perms, memory, 4).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn prepared_runners_instantiate_per_execution_and_free_memory() {
    let r = prepared(Permissions::default(), 24 << 20);
    assert!(r.pooled(), "the pooling allocator is available on the development platforms");
    on_guest_thread(|| {
        for _ in 0..20 {
            // ~16 MiB per execution (the doubling string's copies): only possible 20 times within
            // 24 MiB if every execution starts with fresh memory.
            let out = r.execute("hog", &[Value::Int(8)], ExecIn::default(), &commit_if_ok);
            assert_eq!(out.outcome.result, Ok(Value::Int(8 << 20)));
            assert!(out.mem_bytes >= 16 << 20 && out.fuel > 0, "{} {}", out.mem_bytes, out.fuel);
        }
        let e = r.execute("hog", &[Value::Int(16)], ExecIn::default(), &commit_if_ok).outcome.result.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(e.kind, "out_of_memory");
        assert_eq!(r.execute("quick", &[Value::Int(1)], ExecIn::default(), &commit_if_ok).outcome.result, Ok(Value::Int(2)), "usable after a trap");
        r.instantiate_only().unwrap();
    });
}

#[test]
fn concurrent_executions_keep_their_own_deadlines() {
    let r = prepared(Permissions::default(), 16 << 20).with_fuel(u64::MAX / 4).with_timeout(Duration::from_millis(400));
    std::thread::scope(|s| {
        let slow = std::thread::Builder::new().stack_size(GUEST_THREAD_STACK).spawn_scoped(s, || {
            let t = std::time::Instant::now();
            let e = r.execute("spin", &[Value::Int(0)], ExecIn::default(), &commit_if_ok).outcome.result.unwrap_err();
            (e.kind, t.elapsed())
        });
        let fast = std::thread::Builder::new().stack_size(GUEST_THREAD_STACK).spawn_scoped(s, || {
            let t = std::time::Instant::now();
            let mut n = 0;
            while t.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(900) {
                assert_eq!(r.execute("quick", &[Value::Int(n)], ExecIn::default(), &commit_if_ok).outcome.result, Ok(Value::Int(n + 1)));
                n += 1;
            }
            n
        });
        let (kind, took) = slow.unwrap().join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(kind, "timeout");
        assert!(took >= Duration::from_millis(400) && took < Duration::from_secs(3), "{took:?}");
        assert!(fast.unwrap().join().unwrap() > 10, "short executions are not interrupted by the long one's deadline");
    });
}

#[test]
fn prepared_executions_use_the_callers_database_and_exact_results() {
    let r = prepared(Permissions::parse("db:mem").unwrap(), 16 << 20).with_exact_results(1000);
    on_guest_thread(|| {
        let db = Db::open(&DbGrant::Memory, std::path::Path::new("."), 16).unwrap();
        let out = r.execute("rows", &[], ExecIn { db: Some(db), host_used: 0 }, &commit_if_ok);
        assert_eq!((out.outcome.result, out.outcome.db, out.db.is_some()), (Ok(Value::Int(1)), "commit", true));
        let out = r.execute("quick", &[Value::Int(1)], ExecIn { db: out.db, host_used: 0 }, &commit_if_ok);
        assert!(out.db.is_some(), "an unused connection is handed back");
        assert_eq!(r.execute("text", &[Value::Int(900)], ExecIn::default(), &commit_if_ok).outcome.result, Ok(Value::Str("ab".repeat(450))));
        let e = r.execute("text", &[Value::Int(5000)], ExecIn::default(), &commit_if_ok).outcome.result.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!((e.kind, e.code), ("bad_response", "E0521"));
        // Bytes the caller already holds count against the memory limit.
        let e = r.execute("text", &[Value::Int(10)], ExecIn { db: None, host_used: 17 << 20 }, &commit_if_ok);
        assert!(e.outcome.result.is_ok(), "host_used is only checked when the host buffers more");
    });
}
```

```rust
/// A short text for a database error outside a guest call.
pub fn describe(e: &DbErr) -> String {
    match e {
        DbErr::Constraint(m) | DbErr::Sql(m) => m.clone(),
        DbErr::Busy => format!("the database is busy: another execution held its write lock for more than {} s", BUSY_TIMEOUT.as_secs()),
        DbErr::Full => "the database is full".into(),
        DbErr::TooLarge => "too many rows".into(),
        DbErr::HostOom(n) => format!("{n} bytes exceed the memory limit"),
    }
}
```

with:

```rust
/// Wasm call stack limit; every invocation runs on a thread with a larger native stack.
pub const WASM_STACK_BYTES: usize = 64 << 20;
/// Native stack of a thread that runs guest code (the wasm stack plus room for the host).
pub const GUEST_THREAD_STACK: usize = WASM_STACK_BYTES + (64 << 20);
/// Interval of the epoch ticker of a prepared runner; deadlines are whole ticks.
pub const EPOCH_TICK: Duration = Duration::from_millis(10);

/// What a caller hands one execution of a prepared runner ([`Runner::execute`]).
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct ExecIn {
    /// The connection to run on (a server worker's); the execution's transaction is begun and
    /// ended on it if the function uses `db`. None: opened per execution if needed.
    pub db: Option<Db>,
    /// Host bytes already held for this execution (a request's head and body).
    pub host_used: usize,
}

/// The result of [`Runner::execute`] plus what the caller needs back.
pub struct ExecOut {
    pub outcome: Outcome,
    /// The connection given in [`ExecIn::db`] (or opened), after the transaction ended.
    pub db: Option<Db>,
    /// Fuel the execution used.
    pub fuel: u64,
    /// Size of the guest's linear memory at the end.
    pub mem_bytes: usize,
}

/// Increments an engine's epoch every [`EPOCH_TICK`] until dropped: concurrent executions on one
/// engine each have their own deadline in ticks (a one-shot watchdog would stop them all).
struct Ticker {
    stop: Option<mpsc::Sender<()>>,
    thread: Option<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>>,
}

impl Ticker {
    fn start(engine: Engine) -> Result<Ticker, String> {
        let (stop, wait) = mpsc::channel::<()>();
        let thread = std::thread::Builder::new()
            .name("qal-epoch".into())
            .spawn(move || {
                while let Err(mpsc::RecvTimeoutError::Timeout) = wait.recv_timeout(EPOCH_TICK) {
                    engine.increment_epoch();
                }
            })
            .map_err(|e| format!("cannot start the epoch ticker: {e}"))?;
        Ok(Ticker { stop: Some(stop), thread: Some(thread) })
    }
}

impl Drop for Ticker {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        drop(self.stop.take());
        if let Some(t) = self.thread.take() {
            let _ = t.join();
        }
    }
}

pub struct Runner {
```

```rust
impl Runner {
    pub fn new(meta: CompiledModule) -> Result<Runner, String> {
        let mut config = Config::new();
        config.wasm_tail_call(true);
        config.consume_fuel(true);
        // Interrupts a call that runs longer than its wall-clock timeout (see `with_timeout`).
        config.epoch_interruption(true);
        // Compile time must stay linear in the module size (a 150 000-element list literal took
        // minutes with the optimizing defaults): no optimization passes, single-pass register
        // allocation. Phase 1 programs are bounded by fuel, not by speed.
        config.cranelift_opt_level(wasmtime::OptLevel::None);
        config.cranelift_regalloc_algorithm(wasmtime::RegallocAlgorithm::SinglePass);
        // The unoptimized frames are large: allow ~20 000+ nested non-tail calls; deeper recursion
        // reports `stack_overflow` (`invoke` runs guest code on a thread with a larger native stack).
        config.max_wasm_stack(WASM_STACK_BYTES);
        config.async_stack_size(WASM_STACK_BYTES); // only validated against the limit; calls are synchronous
        let engine = Engine::new(&config).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let module = Module::new(&engine, &meta.wasm).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let types = TProgram { types: meta.types.clone(), ..TProgram::default() };
        Ok(Runner { engine, module, meta, types, fuel: DEFAULT_FUEL, memory_limit: DEFAULT_MEMORY_LIMIT, perms: Permissions::default(), stdin: None, timeout: None, stdout_limit: None, budgets: Limits::default() })
    }

```

```rust
    stdout_limit: Option<usize>,
    budgets: Limits,
    /// Prepared runners: the module linked once (per-execution stores instantiate it).
    pre: Option<InstancePre<HostState>>,
    ticker: Option<Arc<Ticker>>,
    pooled: bool,
    /// Results are decoded exactly up to this many bytes (else with display limits).
    exact_results: Option<usize>,
}

/// The engine settings every runner uses.
fn base_config() -> Config {
    let mut config = Config::new();
    config.wasm_tail_call(true);
    config.consume_fuel(true);
    // Interrupts a call that runs longer than its wall-clock timeout (see `with_timeout`).
    config.epoch_interruption(true);
    // Compile time must stay linear in the module size (a 150 000-element list literal took
    // minutes with the optimizing defaults): no optimization passes, single-pass register
    // allocation. Programs are bounded by fuel, not by speed.
    config.cranelift_opt_level(wasmtime::OptLevel::None);
    config.cranelift_regalloc_algorithm(wasmtime::RegallocAlgorithm::SinglePass);
    // The unoptimized frames are large: allow ~20 000+ nested non-tail calls; deeper recursion
    // reports `stack_overflow` (guest code runs on a thread with a larger native stack).
    config.max_wasm_stack(WASM_STACK_BYTES);
    config.async_stack_size(WASM_STACK_BYTES); // only validated against the limit; calls are synchronous
    config
}

impl Runner {
    pub fn new(meta: CompiledModule) -> Result<Runner, String> {
        let engine = Engine::new(&base_config()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let module = Module::new(&engine, &meta.wasm).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let types = TProgram { types: meta.types.clone(), ..TProgram::default() };
        Ok(Runner { engine, module, meta, types, fuel: DEFAULT_FUEL, memory_limit: DEFAULT_MEMORY_LIMIT, perms: Permissions::default(), stdin: None, timeout: None, stdout_limit: None, budgets: Limits::default(), pre: None, ticker: None, pooled: false, exact_results: None })
    }

    /// A runner for a server (spec Phase 3 §2.2-§2.4): the module is compiled and linked once with
    /// `perms`; every [`Runner::execute`] instantiates it in a fresh store (from wasmtime's pooling
    /// allocator with `slots` instance slots of at most `memory_limit` bytes, falling back to the
    /// on-demand allocator if the pool cannot be set up) and discards it afterwards, so nothing of
    /// one execution survives into the next. Wall-clock limits use an epoch ticker, so concurrent
    /// executions do not affect each other's deadlines.
    pub fn prepared(meta: CompiledModule, perms: Permissions, memory_limit: usize, slots: usize) -> Result<Runner, String> {
        let table = meta.funcs.iter().filter(|f| !f.is_test).count().max(1);
        let mut pool = PoolingAllocationConfig::default();
        let slots = slots.clamp(1, 1024) as u32;
        pool.total_memories(slots).total_core_instances(slots).total_tables(slots);
        pool.max_memories_per_module(1).max_tables_per_module(1).table_elements(table).max_memory_size(memory_limit);
        let mut config = base_config();
        config.allocation_strategy(InstanceAllocationStrategy::Pooling(pool));
        let (engine, pooled) = match Engine::new(&config).and_then(|e| Module::new(&e, &meta.wasm).map(|m| (e, m))) {
            Ok((e, m)) => ((e, m), true),
            Err(_) => {
                let e = Engine::new(&base_config()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                let m = Module::new(&e, &meta.wasm).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                ((e, m), false)
            }
        };
        let (engine, module) = engine;
        let mut linker = Linker::new(&engine);
        host::link(&mut linker, &perms).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let pre = linker.instantiate_pre(&module).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let ticker = Ticker::start(engine.clone())?;
        let types = TProgram { types: meta.types.clone(), ..TProgram::default() };
        Ok(Runner {
            engine,
            module,
            meta,
            types,
            fuel: DEFAULT_FUEL,
            memory_limit,
            perms,
            stdin: Some(Vec::new()),
            timeout: None,
            stdout_limit: None,
            budgets: Limits::default(),
            pre: Some(pre),
            ticker: Some(Arc::new(ticker)),
            pooled,
            exact_results: None,
        })
    }

    /// True if executions use wasmtime's pooling allocator (see [`Runner::prepared`]).
    pub fn pooled(&self) -> bool {
        self.pooled
    }

    /// Decodes results exactly (no shortened strings) up to `bytes`; larger results are `bad_response`.
    pub fn with_exact_results(mut self, bytes: usize) -> Runner {
        self.exact_results = Some(bytes);
        self
    }

    pub fn permissions(&self) -> &Permissions {
        &self.perms
    }

    pub fn memory_limit(&self) -> usize {
        self.memory_limit
    }

    pub fn limits(&self) -> Limits {
        self.budgets
    }

    /// Instantiates the module in a fresh store and discards it (the per-request overhead of a
    /// prepared runner, measured by the performance gate).
    pub fn instantiate_only(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        let mut store = Store::new(&self.engine, HostState::new(self.perms.clone(), Box::new(Cursor::new(Vec::new())), self.memory_limit, self.budgets));
        store.limiter(|s| &mut s.limits);
        store.set_fuel(self.fuel).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        store.set_epoch_deadline(u64::MAX / 2);
        match &self.pre {
            Some(pre) => pre.instantiate(&mut store).map(|_| ()).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
            None => Err("not a prepared runner".into()),
        }
    }
```

```rust
        let watchdog = match (&self.ticker, self.timeout) {
            (Some(_), Some(t)) => {
                // Whole ticks, rounded up, plus one: the current tick may be about to end.
                let ticks = t.as_nanos().div_ceil(EPOCH_TICK.as_nanos()) as u64 + 1;
                store.set_epoch_deadline(ticks);
                None
            }
            (Some(_), None) => {
                store.epoch_deadline_trap();
                store.set_epoch_deadline(u64::MAX / 2);
                None
            }
            (None, t) => {
                // Relative to the engine's current epoch, so ticks of earlier watchdogs do not count.
                store.set_epoch_deadline(1);

                    Ok(w) => w,
                    Err(m) => return early(Outcome::failed(export, m), store.data_mut().db.take()),
                }
            }
        };
        let mut result = self.invoke_in(&mut store, export, args);
        drop(watchdog);
```

```rust
pub fn open_db(grant: &DbGrant, perms: &Permissions, budgets: Limits, def: &str) -> Result<Db, RunError> {
    let mut db = Db::open(grant, &perms.db_dir(), budgets.db_max_mib).map_err(|m| RunError::new("db_open", def, m).hint("the database file is chosen by the host: `db` is .qal/db/main.sqlite, `db:<name>` is .qal/db/<name>.sqlite, `db:mem` is in memory"))?;
    db.begin().map_err(|e| {
        let m = match e {
            db::DbErr::Busy => format!("the database is busy: another execution held its write lock for more than {} s", db::BUSY_TIMEOUT.as_secs()),
            other => format!("cannot start the transaction: {other:?}"),
        };
        RunError::new("timeout", def, m)
    })?;
    Ok(db)
}
```

```rust
pub fn open_db(grant: &DbGrant, perms: &Permissions, budgets: Limits, def: &str) -> Result<Db, RunError> {
    let mut db = Db::open(grant, &perms.db_dir(), budgets.db_max_mib).map_err(|m| RunError::new("db_open", def, m).hint("the database file is chosen by the host: `db` is .qal/db/main.sqlite, `db:<name>` is .qal/db/<name>.sqlite, `db:mem` is in memory"))?;
    db.begin().map_err(|e| RunError::new("timeout", def, format!("cannot start the transaction: {}", db::describe(&e))))?;
    Ok(db)
}
```

```text
# Test fixture for qal-serve (hand-written test code, not the standard library): a small todo
# service plus endpoints that misbehave on purpose.

fn setup() -> Result[Unit, Str] !db = match db_exec("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS todos (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, title TEXT NOT NULL)", []) {
  Ok(_) => Ok(()),
  Err(_) => Err("cannot create the table")
}

fn text(status: Int, body: Str) -> HttpResponse = HttpResponse { status: status, headers: [], body: body }

fn json(status: Int, body: Str) -> HttpResponse = HttpResponse { status: status, headers: [HttpHeader { name: "content-type", value: "application/json" }], body: body }

fn todo_json(id: Int, title: Str) -> Str = "{\"id\":" ++ from_int(id) ++ ",\"title\":\"" ++ title ++ "\"}"

fn row_json(row: List[SqlValue]) -> Str = match get(row, 0) {
  Some(SqlInt(id)) => match get(row, 1) { Some(SqlText(t)) => todo_json(id, t), _ => "null" },
  _ => "null"
}

fn add_row(acc: Str, row: List[SqlValue]) -> Str = if acc == "" then row_json(row) else acc ++ "," ++ row_json(row)

fn create(title: Str) -> HttpResponse !db = if title == "" then text(400, "empty title") else match db_query("INSERT INTO todos (title) VALUES (?) RETURNING id, title", [SqlText(title)]) {
  Ok(r) => match get(r.rows, 0) {
    Some(row) => match get(row, 0) {
      Some(SqlInt(id)) => HttpResponse { status: 201, headers: [HttpHeader { name: "content-type", value: "application/json" }, HttpHeader { name: "location", value: "/todos/" ++ from_int(id) }], body: row_json(row) },
      _ => text(500, "no id")
    },
    None => text(500, "no row")
  },
  Err(_) => text(500, "insert failed")
}

fn list() -> HttpResponse !db = match db_query("SELECT id, title FROM todos ORDER BY id", []) {
  Ok(r) => json(200, "[" ++ fold(r.rows, "", add_row) ++ "]"),
  Err(_) => text(500, "query failed")
}

fn one(method: Str, id: Str) -> HttpResponse !db = match to_int(id) {
  None => text(400, "bad id"),
  Some(n) => if method == "DELETE" then (match db_exec("DELETE FROM todos WHERE id = ?", [SqlInt(n)]) { Ok(0) => text(404, "no such todo"), Ok(_) => text(204, ""), Err(_) => text(500, "delete failed") })
    else match db_query("SELECT id, title FROM todos WHERE id = ?", [SqlInt(n)]) {
      Ok(r) => match get(r.rows, 0) { Some(row) => json(200, row_json(row)), None => text(404, "no such todo") },
      Err(_) => text(500, "query failed")
    }
}

fn grow(s: Str, k: Int) -> Str = if k == 0 then s else grow(s ++ s, k - 1)

fn spin(n: Int) -> Int = spin(n + 1)

fn count(i: Int, n: Int) -> Int = if i >= n then i else count(i + 1, n)

fn hname(h: HttpHeader) -> Str = h.name

fn handle(rq: HttpRequest) -> HttpResponse !db !io = let p = rq.path in
  if p == "/health" then text(200, "ok")
  else if p == "/todos" then (if rq.method == "POST" then create(rq.body) else if rq.method == "GET" then list() else text(405, "method not allowed"))
  else if starts_with(p, "/todos/") then one(rq.method, slice(p, 7, len(p)))
  else if p == "/echo" then text(200, rq.method ++ " " ++ p ++ "?" ++ rq.query ++ " " ++ join(map(rq.headers, hname), ",") ++ " " ++ rq.body)
  else if p == "/trap" then text(200, from_int(9223372036854775807 + len(p)))
  else if p == "/spin" then text(200, from_int(spin(0)))
  else if p == "/big" then (if grow("0123456789abcdef", 20) == "" then text(500, "") else text(200, "16777216"))
  else if p == "/bad" then HttpResponse { status: 200, headers: [HttpHeader { name: "x-split", value: "a\r\nset-cookie: evil" }], body: "" }
  else if p == "/print" then { print("hello from the handler"); text(200, "printed") }
  else if p == "/slow" then text(200, from_int(count(0, match to_int(rq.query) { Some(n) => n, None => 1000 })))
  else if p == "/fail" then (match db_exec("INSERT INTO todos (title) VALUES (?)", [SqlText("rolled back")]) { Ok(_) => text(422, "rejected after writing"), Err(_) => text(500, "insert failed") })
  else text(404, "not found")
```

Create `qal/crates/qal-serve/tests/serve.rs`:

```rust
//! The server over real TCP connections, with the fixture service `fixtures/app.qal`.

use qal_run::Permissions;
use qal_serve::{bind, serve, HttpLimits, Log, ServerConfig, Service, ServiceConfig};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::{SocketAddr, TcpStream};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const APP: &str = include_str!("fixtures/app.qal");
static NEXT: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);

fn typed() -> qal_core::tast::TProgram {
    qal_check::check(&qal_text::parse_program(APP).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

fn project() -> PathBuf {
    let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("qal-serve-test-{}-{}", std::process::id(), NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst)));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&d).unwrap();
    d
}

/// Collects log lines.
#[derive(Clone, Default)]
struct Lines(Arc<Mutex<Vec<u8>>>);

impl Write for Lines {
    fn write(&mut self, b: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
        self.0.lock().unwrap().extend_from_slice(b);
        Ok(b.len())
    }
    fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Lines {
    fn events(&self) -> Vec<Value> {
        String::from_utf8(self.0.lock().unwrap().clone()).unwrap().lines().map(|l| serde_json::from_str(l).unwrap()).collect()
    }
}

struct Running {
    addr: SocketAddr,
    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    thread: Option<std::thread::JoinHandle<u64>>,
    log: Lines,
}

impl Running {
    fn stop(mut self) -> u64 {
        self.shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
        self.thread.take().unwrap().join().unwrap()
    }
}

impl Drop for Running {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
        if let Some(t) = self.thread.take() {
            let _ = t.join();
        }
    }
}

fn config(dir: &std::path::Path) -> (Permissions, ServiceConfig, ServerConfig) {
    let perms = Permissions::parse("db,io").unwrap().with_db_dir(dir.join(".qal/db"));
    let svc = ServiceConfig { init: Some("setup".into()), request_timeout: Duration::from_millis(1500), memory: 40 << 20, ..ServiceConfig::default() };
    let http = HttpLimits { header_timeout: Duration::from_millis(500), body_timeout: Duration::from_millis(500), ..HttpLimits::default() };
    (perms, svc, ServerConfig { workers: 4, queue: 64, http })
}

fn start_with(perms: Permissions, svc: ServiceConfig, srv: ServerConfig) -> Running {
    let slots = srv.workers + 2;
    let service = Arc::new(Service::new(&typed(), perms, ServiceConfig { slots, ..svc }).unwrap());
    let mut workers: Vec<_> = (0..srv.workers).map(|_| service.worker().unwrap()).collect();
    service.init(&mut workers[0]).unwrap();
    let listener = bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
    let log = Lines::default();
    let shutdown = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
    let (s, l) = (shutdown.clone(), Arc::new(Log::new(Box::new(log.clone()))));
    let thread = std::thread::spawn(move || serve(listener, service, srv, l, s, workers));
    Running { addr, shutdown, thread: Some(thread), log }
}

fn start(dir: &std::path::Path) -> Running {
    let (p, s, h) = config(dir);
    start_with(p, s, h)
}

/// A parsed response.
#[derive(Debug)]
struct Resp {
    status: u16,
    headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
    body: String,
}

impl Resp {
    fn header(&self, n: &str) -> Option<&str> {
        self.headers.iter().find(|(k, _)| k == n).map(|(_, v)| v.as_str())
    }
}

/// Reads one response from `s` (bytes already read past it stay in `rest`).
fn read_resp(s: &mut TcpStream, rest: &mut Vec<u8>, head_only: bool) -> Option<Resp> {
    let mut chunk = [0u8; 8192];
    let end = loop {
        if let Some(i) = rest.windows(4).position(|w| w == b"\r\n\r\n") {
            break i;
        }
        let n = s.read(&mut chunk).ok()?;
        if n == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        rest.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);
    };
    let head = String::from_utf8(rest[..end].to_vec()).unwrap();
    rest.drain(..end + 4);
    let mut lines = head.split("\r\n");
    let status: u16 = lines.next()?.split(' ').nth(1)?.parse().ok()?;
    let headers: Vec<(String, String)> = lines.filter_map(|l| l.split_once(": ").map(|(a, b)| (a.to_string(), b.to_string()))).collect();
    let len: usize = if head_only { 0 } else { headers.iter().find(|(k, _)| k == "content-length").map_or(0, |(_, v)| v.parse().unwrap()) };
    while rest.len() < len {
        let n = s.read(&mut chunk).ok()?;
        if n == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        rest.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);
    }
    let body = String::from_utf8(rest.drain(..len).collect()).unwrap();
    Some(Resp { status, headers, body })
}

fn connect(addr: SocketAddr) -> TcpStream {
    let s = TcpStream::connect(addr).unwrap();
    s.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10))).unwrap();
    s
}

/// One request on a fresh connection.
fn req(addr: SocketAddr, method: &str, path: &str, body: &str) -> Resp {
    let mut s = connect(addr);
    write!(s, "{method} {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len()).unwrap();
    read_resp(&mut s, &mut Vec::new(), method == "HEAD").unwrap()
}

fn raw(addr: SocketAddr, bytes: &[u8]) -> (Option<Resp>, bool) {
    let mut s = connect(addr);
    s.write_all(bytes).unwrap();
    let mut rest = Vec::new();
    let r = read_resp(&mut s, &mut rest, false);
    let mut more = [0u8; 16];
    let closed = matches!(s.read(&mut more), Ok(0));
    (r, closed)
}

#[test]
fn crud_over_keep_alive_and_pipelined_requests() {
    let dir = project();
    let server = start(&dir);
    let mut s = connect(server.addr);
    let mut rest = Vec::new();
    // Three requests written at once are answered in order on the same connection.
    s.write_all(b"POST /todos HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nContent-Length: 4\r\n\r\nmilkPOST /todos HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nContent-Length: 5\r\n\r\nbreadGET /todos HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
    let a = read_resp(&mut s, &mut rest, false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((a.status, a.header("location"), a.body.as_str()), (201, Some("/todos/1"), r#"{"id":1,"title":"milk"}"#));
    assert_eq!(a.header("content-type"), Some("application/json"));
    assert_eq!(a.header("connection"), Some("keep-alive"));
    let b = read_resp(&mut s, &mut rest, false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(b.status, 201);
    let c = read_resp(&mut s, &mut rest, false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(c.body, r#"[{"id":1,"title":"milk"},{"id":2,"title":"bread"}]"#);
    let ids: Vec<u64> = [&a, &b, &c].iter().map(|r| r.header("x-request-id").unwrap().parse().unwrap()).collect();
    assert!(ids[0] < ids[1] && ids[1] < ids[2], "monotonic request ids {ids:?}");
    write!(s, "DELETE /todos/1 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
    let d = read_resp(&mut s, &mut rest, false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((d.status, d.header("content-length")), (204, None), "no content-length for 204");
    write!(s, "GET /todos/1 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
    let e = read_resp(&mut s, &mut rest, false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((e.status, e.header("connection"), e.header("content-type")), (404, Some("close"), Some("text/plain; charset=utf-8")));
    assert_eq!(s.read(&mut [0u8; 8]).unwrap(), 0, "closed after `Connection: close`");
    // HEAD is a GET without the body.
    let h = req(server.addr, "HEAD", "/todos", "");
    assert_eq!((h.status, h.body.as_str()), (200, ""));
    assert_eq!(h.header("content-length"), Some(r#"[{"id":2,"title":"bread"}]"#.len().to_string().as_str()));
    let echo = req(server.addr, "PUT", "/echo?a=1&b=%20", "x");
    assert_eq!(echo.body, "PUT /echo?a=1&b=%20 host,content-length x");
    drop(s);
    let served = server.stop();
    assert!(served >= 7, "{served}");
}

#[test]
fn every_request_runs_in_a_fresh_instance() {
    let dir = project();
    let server = start(&dir);
    let trap = req(server.addr, "GET", "/trap", "");
    assert_eq!(trap.status, 500);
    let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(&trap.body).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(v["error"]["kind"], "internal_error");
    assert_eq!(v["error"]["id"].as_str(), trap.header("x-request-id"));
    assert_eq!(req(server.addr, "GET", "/health", "").body, "ok", "the next request is fine");
    let t = Instant::now();
    let spin = req(server.addr, "GET", "/spin", "");
    assert_eq!(spin.status, 503);
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(6), "{:?}", t.elapsed());
    assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&spin.body).unwrap()["error"]["kind"], "timeout");
    // About 32 MiB per request within a 40 MiB limit, 20 times: memory is freed after each request.
    for _ in 0..20 {
        let big = req(server.addr, "GET", "/big", "");
        assert_eq!((big.status, big.body.as_str()), (200, "16777216"));
    }
    assert_eq!(req(server.addr, "GET", "/bad", "").status, 500, "CR/LF in a header value is a bad_response");
    assert_eq!(req(server.addr, "GET", "/print", "").body, "printed");
    let events = server.log.events();
    let kinds: Vec<&str> = events.iter().filter_map(|e| e["error"]["kind"].as_str()).collect();
    assert_eq!(kinds, vec!["int_overflow", "timeout", "bad_response"]);
    let printed = events.iter().find(|e| e["path"] == "/print").unwrap();
    assert_eq!(printed["out"], "hello from the handler\n");
    let big = events.iter().find(|e| e["path"] == "/big").unwrap();
    assert!(big["mem_bytes"].as_u64().unwrap() >= 32 << 20 && big["fuel"].as_u64().unwrap() > 0 && big["ms"].is_u64(), "{big}");
}

#[test]
fn writes_are_committed_only_for_successful_responses() {
    let dir = project();
    let server = start(&dir);
    assert_eq!(req(server.addr, "POST", "/todos", "kept").status, 201);
    assert_eq!(req(server.addr, "POST", "/fail", "").status, 422);
    assert_eq!(req(server.addr, "GET", "/todos", "").body, r#"[{"id":1,"title":"kept"}]"#, "the 4xx response rolled its insert back");
    let events = server.log.events();
    let dbs: Vec<&str> = events.iter().filter_map(|e| e["db"].as_str()).collect();
    assert_eq!(dbs, vec!["commit", "rollback", "commit"]);
    drop(server);
    // Persistence: a new server on the same database sees the data.
    let again = start(&dir);
    assert_eq!(req(again.addr, "GET", "/todos/1", "").body, r#"{"id":1,"title":"kept"}"#);
}

#[test]
fn concurrent_posts_get_distinct_ids() {
    let dir = project();
    let server = start(&dir);
    let addr = server.addr;
    let threads: Vec<_> = (0..50).map(|i| std::thread::spawn(move || req(addr, "POST", "/todos", &format!("t{i}")))).collect();
    let mut ids: Vec<String> = threads.into_iter().map(|t| t.join().unwrap()).map(|r| {
        assert_eq!(r.status, 201, "{}", r.body);
        r.header("location").unwrap().to_string()
    }).collect();
    ids.sort();
    ids.dedup();
    assert_eq!(ids.len(), 50);
    let list = req(addr, "GET", "/todos", "");
    assert_eq!(list.body.matches("\"id\"").count(), 50);
}

#[test]
fn the_host_refuses_bad_requests_and_closes() {
    let dir = project();
    let server = start(&dir);
    let big = format!("POST /todos HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n", (1 << 20) + 1);
    let cases: Vec<(Vec<u8>, u16, &str)> = vec![
        (big.into_bytes(), 413, "payload_too_large"),
        (format!("GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nX: {}\r\n\r\n", "a".repeat(17 << 10)).into_bytes(), 431, "header_too_large"),
        (b"POST /todos HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n4\r\nmilk\r\n0\r\n\r\n".to_vec(), 411, "length_required"),
        (b"POST /todos HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n\xc3\x28".to_vec(), 400, "bad_request"),
    ];
    for (bytes, status, kind) in cases {
        let (r, closed) = raw(server.addr, &bytes);
        let r = r.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.status, status);
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&r.body).unwrap()["error"]["kind"], kind);
        assert!(closed, "{status}: the connection is closed after a rejection");
    }
    // Slowloris: a head that never ends is cut off after the header timeout.
    let t = Instant::now();
    let mut s = connect(server.addr);
    s.write_all(b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\n").unwrap();
    let r = read_resp(&mut s, &mut Vec::new(), false).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(r.status, 408);
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(4), "{:?}", t.elapsed());
    assert_eq!(req(server.addr, "GET", "/todos", "").body, "[]", "nothing was stored");
    let events = server.log.events();
    assert!(events.iter().filter(|e| e["error"]["code"] == "E0800").count() >= 5, "{events:?}");
}

#[test]
fn a_full_queue_answers_503_and_shutdown_finishes_running_requests() {
    let dir = project();
    let (p, mut svc, mut srv) = config(&dir);
    svc.request_timeout = Duration::from_secs(5);
    svc.fuel = u64::MAX / 4;
    srv.workers = 1;
    srv.queue = 1;
    let server = start_with(p, svc, srv);
    let addr = server.addr;
    // The only worker is busy for a while; one connection fits the queue; the next is refused.
    let slow = std::thread::spawn(move || req(addr, "GET", "/slow?100000000", ""));
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300));
    let mut queued = connect(addr);
    queued.write_all(b"GET /health HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
    let (r, _) = raw(addr, b"GET /health HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: t\r\n\r\n");
    let r = r.unwrap();
    assert_eq!((r.status, r.header("retry-after")), (503, Some("1")));
    assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&r.body).unwrap()["error"]["kind"], "overloaded");
    // Shutdown while the slow request runs: it is still answered, and serve returns.
    server.shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let done = slow.join().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(done.status, 200);
    assert_eq!(read_resp(&mut queued, &mut Vec::new(), false).map(|r| r.status), Some(200), "queued connections are served");
    let log = server.log.clone();
    let served = server.stop();
    assert!(served >= 3, "{served}");
    assert!(log.events().iter().any(|e| e["error"]["code"] == "E0801"));
}

#[test]
fn services_are_checked_before_they_start() {
    let typed = typed();
    let perms = Permissions::parse("db,io").unwrap();
    let e = Service::new(&typed, perms.clone(), ServiceConfig { entry: "text".into(), ..ServiceConfig::default() }).err().unwrap();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, e.code), ("bad_entry", "E0520"));
    assert!(e.message.contains("must be `fn(HttpRequest) -> HttpResponse` (any effects), found `fn(Int, Str) -> HttpResponse`"), "{}", e.message);
    let e = Service::new(&typed, perms.clone(), ServiceConfig { entry: "nope".into(), ..ServiceConfig::default() }).err().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.kind, "bad_entry");
    let e = Service::new(&typed, perms.clone(), ServiceConfig { init: Some("list".into()), ..ServiceConfig::default() }).err().unwrap();
    assert!(e.message.contains("init function `list` must be `fn() -> Result[Unit, Str]`"), "{}", e.message);
    let e = Service::new(&typed, Permissions::parse("io").unwrap(), ServiceConfig::default()).err().unwrap();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, e.hints.clone()), ("permission_denied", vec!["grant them with `--allow db`".to_string()]));
    // An init function that returns Err stops the start.
    let src = format!("{APP}\nfn broken() -> Result[Unit, Str] = Err(\"no\")");
    let typed = qal_check::check(&qal_text::parse_program(&src).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let svc = Service::new(&typed, Permissions::parse("db:mem,io").unwrap(), ServiceConfig { init: Some("broken".into()), ..ServiceConfig::default() }).unwrap();
    let e = svc.init(&mut svc.worker().unwrap()).unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!((e.kind, e.code, e.message.as_str()), ("init_failed", "E0524", "`broken` returned Err: no"));
    assert_eq!(bind("256.0.0.1:1").unwrap_err().code, "E0802");
}

#[test]
fn debug_errors_return_the_runtime_error() {
    let dir = project();
    let (p, mut svc, h) = config(&dir);
    svc.debug_errors = true;
    let server = start_with(p, svc, h);
    let r = req(server.addr, "GET", "/trap", "");
    let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(&r.body).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((v["error"]["kind"].as_str(), v["error"]["code"].as_str()), (Some("int_overflow"), Some("E0502")));
}

/// Spec Phase 3 §2.2: instantiating and discarding the module per request must cost at most
/// 200 µs on average in a release build. Run with `cargo test --release -p qal-serve --test serve
/// instance_overhead -- --nocapture` to see the measurement.
#[test]
fn instance_overhead_meets_the_gate() {
    let svc = Service::new(&typed(), Permissions::parse("db:mem,io").unwrap(), ServiceConfig::default()).unwrap();
    let r = svc.runner();
    for _ in 0..100 {
        r.instantiate_only().unwrap();
    }
    let n = 2000;
    let t = Instant::now();
    for _ in 0..n {
        r.instantiate_only().unwrap();
    }
    let mean = t.elapsed() / n;
    println!("instance overhead: mean {mean:?} over {n} instantiations (pooling allocator: {})", svc.pooled());
    assert!(svc.pooled());
    let gate = if cfg!(debug_assertions) { Duration::from_millis(2) } else { Duration::from_micros(200) };
    assert!(mean <= gate, "mean {mean:?} > {gate:?}");
}
```
