Create `qal/crates/qal-serve/src/http.rs`:

```rust
//! HTTP/1.1 and 1.0 on a TCP connection (spec Phase 3 §2.3, §2.5): reading requests (request line
//! and headers with `httparse`, bodies only with `Content-Length`) under size limits and deadlines,
//! and writing responses (always with `Content-Length`, except for statuses without a body).
//!
//! Everything ambiguous is refused and the connection closed: `Transfer-Encoding` (411 for
//! `chunked`, else 501), several `Content-Length` headers or a malformed one (400), a target that
//! is not origin-form (400), header names with whitespace before the colon or folded lines (the
//! parser refuses them). Requests on one connection are read and answered strictly in order.

use crate::service::{bodiless, Request, Response};
use std::io::{ErrorKind, Read, Write};
use std::net::TcpStream;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Limits of the HTTP layer.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct HttpLimits {
    /// Request line plus headers.
    pub max_header_bytes: usize,
    pub max_headers: usize,
    pub max_body: usize,
    /// Time for the request line and headers, from the connection's (or the request's first byte's) start.
    pub header_timeout: Duration,
    /// Time for the body, from the end of the headers.
    pub body_timeout: Duration,
    /// Requests per connection.
    pub keepalive_requests: usize,
    /// Idle time between requests on one connection.
    pub keepalive_idle: Duration,
}

impl Default for HttpLimits {
    fn default() -> HttpLimits {
        HttpLimits {
            max_header_bytes: 16 << 10,
            max_headers: 64,
            max_body: 1 << 20,
            header_timeout: Duration::from_secs(5),
            body_timeout: Duration::from_secs(10),
            keepalive_requests: 100,
            keepalive_idle: Duration::from_secs(5),
        }
    }
}

/// A request the host refuses without calling the handler.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Rejection {
    pub status: u16,
    pub kind: &'static str,
    pub message: String,
}

impl Rejection {
    fn new(status: u16, message: impl Into<String>) -> Rejection {
        let kind = match status {
            400 => "bad_request",
            408 => "request_timeout",
            411 => "length_required",
            413 => "payload_too_large",
            431 => "header_too_large",
            501 => "not_implemented",
            _ => "rejected",
        };
        Rejection { status, kind, message: message.into() }
    }

    /// The response for this rejection (JSON body).
    pub fn response(&self, id: u64) -> Response {
        let body = serde_json::json!({"error": {"kind": self.kind, "message": self.message}}).to_string();
        Response::json(self.status, body, id)
    }
}

/// A request read from a connection.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Incoming {
    pub request: Request,
    /// Size of the request line and headers as received.
    pub head_bytes: usize,
    /// The client allows the connection to stay open.
    pub keep_alive: bool,
}

/// What reading the next request gave.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Next {
    Request(Box<Incoming>),
    /// The client closed the connection or stayed idle between requests: close without a response.
    Closed,
    /// Answer with this rejection, then close.
    Reject(Rejection),
}

/// Hop-by-hop headers: they describe this connection, not the request (removed for the handler).
const HOP_BY_HOP: [&str; 6] = ["connection", "keep-alive", "transfer-encoding", "upgrade", "te", "trailer"];

/// One client connection with the bytes read ahead (pipelined requests).
pub struct Conn {
    stream: TcpStream,
    buf: Vec<u8>,
    /// Responses written so far.
    pub answered: usize,
}

fn reason(status: u16) -> &'static str {
    match status {
        100 => "Continue",
        101 => "Switching Protocols",
        200 => "OK",
        201 => "Created",
        202 => "Accepted",
        204 => "No Content",
        301 => "Moved Permanently",
        302 => "Found",
        303 => "See Other",
        304 => "Not Modified",
        307 => "Temporary Redirect",
        308 => "Permanent Redirect",
        400 => "Bad Request",
        401 => "Unauthorized",
        403 => "Forbidden",
        404 => "Not Found",
        405 => "Method Not Allowed",
        408 => "Request Timeout",
        409 => "Conflict",
        411 => "Length Required",
        413 => "Content Too Large",
        415 => "Unsupported Media Type",
        422 => "Unprocessable Content",
        429 => "Too Many Requests",
        431 => "Request Header Fields Too Large",
        500 => "Internal Server Error",
        501 => "Not Implemented",
        502 => "Bad Gateway",
        503 => "Service Unavailable",
        504 => "Gateway Timeout",
        _ => "Status",
    }
}

/// The bytes of a response (`head_only`: without the body, for HEAD).
pub fn encode_response(resp: &Response, head_only: bool, keep_alive: bool) -> Vec<u8> {
    let mut out = format!("HTTP/1.1 {} {}\r\n", resp.status, reason(resp.status));
    for (n, v) in &resp.headers {
        out.push_str(n);
        out.push_str(": ");
        out.push_str(v);
        out.push_str("\r\n");
    }
    out.push_str(if keep_alive { "connection: keep-alive\r\n\r\n" } else { "connection: close\r\n\r\n" });
    let mut bytes = out.into_bytes();
    if !head_only && !bodiless(resp.status) {
        bytes.extend_from_slice(resp.body.as_bytes());
    }
    bytes
}

impl Conn {
    pub fn new(stream: TcpStream) -> Conn {
        let _ = stream.set_nodelay(true);
        let _ = stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)));
        Conn { stream, buf: Vec::new(), answered: 0 }
    }

    /// Reads into the buffer until `deadline`. Ok(false): the peer closed; Err: timed out.
    fn fill(&mut self, deadline: Instant) -> Result<bool, ()> {
        let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now());
        if left.is_zero() {
            return Err(());
        }
        let _ = self.stream.set_read_timeout(Some(left));
        let mut chunk = [0u8; 8192];
        loop {
            match self.stream.read(&mut chunk) {
                Ok(0) => return Ok(false),
                Ok(n) => {
                    self.buf.extend_from_slice(&chunk[..n]);
                    return Ok(true);
                }
                Err(e) if e.kind() == ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
                Err(e) if matches!(e.kind(), ErrorKind::WouldBlock | ErrorKind::TimedOut) => return Err(()),
                Err(_) => return Ok(false),
            }
        }
    }

    /// Reads the next request. `start` is when the connection was accepted (first request); for
    /// later requests the header deadline starts with their first byte, after at most
    /// `keepalive_idle` of waiting.
    pub fn next(&mut self, limits: &HttpLimits, start: Instant) -> Next {
        let first = self.answered == 0;
        if self.buf.is_empty() && !first {
            match self.fill(Instant::now() + limits.keepalive_idle) {
                Ok(true) => {}
                Ok(false) | Err(()) => return Next::Closed,
            }
        }
        let head_deadline = if first { start } else { Instant::now() } + limits.header_timeout;
        let head_len = loop {
            let mut headers = vec![httparse::EMPTY_HEADER; limits.max_headers];
            let mut req = httparse::Request::new(&mut headers);
            match req.parse(&self.buf) {
                Ok(httparse::Status::Complete(n)) => break n,
                Ok(httparse::Status::Partial) => {}
                Err(httparse::Error::TooManyHeaders) => return Next::Reject(Rejection::new(431, format!("more than {} header fields", limits.max_headers))),
                Err(e) => return Next::Reject(Rejection::new(400, format!("malformed request: {e}"))),
            }
            if self.buf.len() > limits.max_header_bytes {
                return Next::Reject(Rejection::new(431, format!("the request line and headers are larger than {} bytes", limits.max_header_bytes)));
            }
            match self.fill(head_deadline) {
                Ok(true) => {}
                // The client closed the connection before the request was complete.
                Ok(false) => return Next::Closed,
                Err(()) => return Next::Reject(Rejection::new(408, format!("the request line and headers did not arrive within {} ms", limits.header_timeout.as_millis()))),
            }
        };
        if head_len > limits.max_header_bytes {
            return Next::Reject(Rejection::new(431, format!("the request line and headers are larger than {} bytes", limits.max_header_bytes)));
        }
        let parsed = match self.head(limits, head_len) {
            Ok(p) => p,
            Err(r) => return Next::Reject(r),
        };
        let (mut incoming, body_len, expect) = parsed;
        self.buf.drain(..head_len);
        if expect && self.buf.len() < body_len {
            let _ = self.stream.write_all(b"HTTP/1.1 100 Continue\r\n\r\n");
        }
        let body_deadline = Instant::now() + limits.body_timeout;
        while self.buf.len() < body_len {
            match self.fill(body_deadline) {
                Ok(true) => {}
                Ok(false) => return Next::Closed,
                Err(()) => return Next::Reject(Rejection::new(408, format!("the body did not arrive within {} ms", limits.body_timeout.as_millis()))),
            }
        }
        let body: Vec<u8> = self.buf.drain(..body_len).collect();
        match String::from_utf8(body) {
            Ok(b) => incoming.request.body = b,
            Err(_) => return Next::Reject(Rejection::new(400, "the body is not valid UTF-8")),
        }
        Next::Request(Box::new(incoming))
    }

    /// Interprets the parsed head: the request without its body, the body length, and whether
    /// the client waits for `100 Continue`.
    fn head(&self, limits: &HttpLimits, head_len: usize) -> Result<(Incoming, usize, bool), Rejection> {
        let mut headers = vec![httparse::EMPTY_HEADER; limits.max_headers];
        let mut req = httparse::Request::new(&mut headers);
        if !matches!(req.parse(&self.buf), Ok(httparse::Status::Complete(_))) {
            return Err(Rejection::new(400, "malformed request"));
        }
        let method = req.method.unwrap_or("").to_string();
        let target = req.path.unwrap_or("");
        let version = req.version.unwrap_or(1);
        if !target.starts_with('/') {
            return Err(Rejection::new(400, "the request target must be a path starting with `/` (no absolute-form or `*`)"));
        }
        let (path, query) = match target.split_once('?') {
            Some((p, q)) => (p.to_string(), q.to_string()),
            None => (target.to_string(), String::new()),
        };
        let mut list = Vec::new();
        let mut length: Option<usize> = None;
        let (mut te, mut close, mut keep, mut expect, mut host) = (None::<String>, false, false, false, false);
        for h in req.headers.iter() {
            let name = h.name.to_ascii_lowercase();
            let value = std::str::from_utf8(h.value).map_err(|_| Rejection::new(400, format!("the value of header `{name}` is not UTF-8")))?.trim().to_string();
            match name.as_str() {
                "content-length" => {
                    if length.is_some() {
                        return Err(Rejection::new(400, "more than one Content-Length header"));
                    }
                    if value.is_empty() || value.len() > 18 || !value.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
                        return Err(Rejection::new(400, format!("invalid Content-Length `{}`", value.chars().take(40).collect::<String>())));
                    }
                    length = Some(value.parse().map_err(|_| Rejection::new(400, "invalid Content-Length"))?);
                }
                "transfer-encoding" => te = Some(value.to_ascii_lowercase()),
                "connection" => {
                    for token in value.split(',').map(|t| t.trim().to_ascii_lowercase()) {
                        close |= token == "close";
                        keep |= token == "keep-alive";
                    }
                }
                "expect" => expect |= value.eq_ignore_ascii_case("100-continue"),
                "host" => host = true,
                _ => {}
            }
            if !HOP_BY_HOP.contains(&name.as_str()) {
                list.push((name, value));
            }
        }
        if let Some(te) = te {
            let last = te.rsplit(',').next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_string();
            return Err(if last == "chunked" { Rejection::new(411, "chunked request bodies are not supported: send Content-Length") } else { Rejection::new(501, format!("Transfer-Encoding `{te}` is not supported")) });
        }
        if version == 1 && !host {
            return Err(Rejection::new(400, "HTTP/1.1 requests need a Host header"));
        }
        let body_len = length.unwrap_or(0);
        if body_len > limits.max_body {
            return Err(Rejection::new(413, format!("the body has {body_len} bytes, more than the limit of {}", limits.max_body)));
        }
        let keep_alive = if version == 1 { !close } else { keep && !close };
        let request = Request { method, path, query, headers: list, body: String::new() };
        Ok((Incoming { request, head_bytes: head_len, keep_alive }, body_len, expect && version == 1))
    }

    /// Writes a response.
    pub fn send(&mut self, resp: &Response, head_only: bool, keep_alive: bool) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        self.answered += 1;
        self.stream.write_all(&encode_response(resp, head_only, keep_alive))?;
        self.stream.flush()
    }

    pub fn stream(&self) -> &TcpStream {
        &self.stream
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::net::TcpListener;

    /// A connection whose client side sends `chunks` (with `pause` between them) and keeps the
    /// socket open for `hold`.
    fn conn(chunks: Vec<Vec<u8>>, pause: Duration) -> (Conn, std::thread::JoinHandle<Vec<u8>>) {
        let l = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
        let addr = l.local_addr().unwrap();
        let client = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut s = TcpStream::connect(addr).unwrap();
            for c in chunks {
                s.write_all(&c).unwrap();
                std::thread::sleep(pause);
            }
            let _ = s.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)));
            let mut out = Vec::new();
            let _ = s.read_to_end(&mut out);
            out
        });
        let (s, _) = l.accept().unwrap();
        (Conn::new(s), client)
    }

    fn limits() -> HttpLimits {
        HttpLimits { header_timeout: Duration::from_millis(400), body_timeout: Duration::from_millis(400), keepalive_idle: Duration::from_millis(300), ..HttpLimits::default() }
    }

    fn one(raw: &[u8]) -> Next {
        let (mut c, _client) = conn(vec![raw.to_vec()], Duration::ZERO);
        c.next(&limits(), Instant::now())
    }

    fn rejected(raw: &[u8]) -> (u16, String) {
        match one(raw) {
            Next::Reject(r) => (r.status, r.message),
            other => panic!("{other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn requests_are_parsed_with_lowercase_headers_and_no_hop_by_hop_headers() {
        let Next::Request(i) = one(b"POST /todos/1?done=true&x=%20 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nX-Thing:  v  \r\nConnection: keep-alive, Upgrade\r\nUpgrade: h2c\r\nContent-Length: 4\r\n\r\nbody") else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(i.request.method, "POST");
        assert_eq!((i.request.path.as_str(), i.request.query.as_str()), ("/todos/1", "done=true&x=%20"));
        assert_eq!(i.request.headers, vec![("host".to_string(), "a".to_string()), ("x-thing".into(), "v".into()), ("content-length".into(), "4".into())]);
        assert_eq!((i.request.body.as_str(), i.keep_alive), ("body", true));
        let Next::Request(i) = one(b"GET / HTTP/1.0\r\n\r\n") else { panic!() };
        assert!(!i.keep_alive, "HTTP/1.0 closes unless keep-alive is asked for");
        let Next::Request(i) = one(b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n") else { panic!() };
        assert!(!i.keep_alive);
    }

    #[test]
    fn ambiguous_or_oversized_requests_are_refused() {
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n0\r\n\r\n").0, 411);
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nContent-Length: 3\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n").0, 411, "CL + TE: smuggling");
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nTransfer-Encoding: gzip\r\n\r\n").0, 501);
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nContent-Length: 3\r\nContent-Length: 3\r\n\r\nabc").0, 400);
        for cl in ["+3", "-1", "3 3", "0x3", "3,3", "99999999999999999999"] {
            let raw = format!("POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nContent-Length: {cl}\r\n\r\nabc");
            assert_eq!(rejected(raw.as_bytes()).0, 400, "{cl}");
        }
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nContent-Length : 3\r\n\r\nabc").0, 400, "space before the colon");
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nX: a\r\n b\r\n\r\n").0, 400, "folded header");
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"GET http://evil/ HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\n\r\n").0, 400);
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"OPTIONS * HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\n\r\n").0, 400);
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n").0, 400, "no Host");
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"PRI * HTTP/2.0\r\n\r\nSM\r\n\r\n").0, 400);
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"G@T / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\n\r\n").0, 400);
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n\xff\xfe").0, 400);
        assert_eq!(rejected(b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nX-Bin: \xff\r\n\r\n").0, 400);
        let big = format!("POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n", (1 << 20) + 1);
        assert_eq!(rejected(big.as_bytes()).0, 413);
        let many: String = (0..65).map(|i| format!("X-{i}: v\r\n")).collect();
        assert_eq!(rejected(format!("GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\n{many}\r\n").as_bytes()).0, 431);
        let long = format!("GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nX: {}\r\n\r\n", "v".repeat(17 << 10));
        assert_eq!(rejected(long.as_bytes()).0, 431);
    }

    #[test]
    fn slow_clients_time_out() {
        let t = Instant::now();
        let (mut c, _client) = conn(vec![b"GET / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\n".to_vec(), b"X-Slow: 1\r\n".to_vec()], Duration::from_millis(250));
        let Next::Reject(r) = c.next(&limits(), Instant::now()) else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(r.status, 408);
        assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(1));
        let (mut c, _client) = conn(vec![b"POST / HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nContent-Length: 10\r\n\r\nabc".to_vec()], Duration::from_millis(900));
        let Next::Reject(r) = c.next(&limits(), Instant::now()) else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!((r.status, r.message.contains("body")), (408, true));
    }

    #[test]
    fn pipelined_requests_are_read_in_order_and_idle_connections_close() {
        let two = b"GET /a HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\n\r\nPOST /b HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\nhiGET /c HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\n\r\n".to_vec();
        let (mut c, client) = conn(vec![two], Duration::ZERO);
        let mut paths = Vec::new();
        for _ in 0..3 {
            let Next::Request(i) = c.next(&limits(), Instant::now()) else { panic!() };
            paths.push((i.request.path.clone(), i.request.body.clone()));
            c.send(&Response::json(200, "{}".into(), 1), false, true).unwrap();
        }
        assert_eq!(paths, vec![("/a".into(), "".into()), ("/b".into(), "hi".into()), ("/c".into(), "".into())]);
        assert_eq!(c.next(&limits(), Instant::now()), Next::Closed, "idle after the last request");
        drop(c);
        let out = String::from_utf8(client.join().unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(out.matches("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\n").count(), 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn expect_continue_is_answered_before_the_body() {
        let l = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
        let addr = l.local_addr().unwrap();
        let client = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut s = TcpStream::connect(addr).unwrap();
            s.write_all(b"PUT /x HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: a\r\nExpect: 100-continue\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
            let mut buf = [0u8; 25];
            s.read_exact(&mut buf).unwrap();
            s.write_all(b"ok").unwrap();
            String::from_utf8(buf.to_vec()).unwrap()
        });
        let (s, _) = l.accept().unwrap();
        let mut c = Conn::new(s);
        let Next::Request(i) = c.next(&limits(), Instant::now()) else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(i.request.body, "ok");
        assert_eq!(client.join().unwrap(), "HTTP/1.1 100 Continue\r\n\r\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn responses_always_carry_their_length() {
        let r = Response { status: 201, headers: vec![("content-type".into(), "text/plain".into()), ("content-length".into(), "2".into())], body: "hi".into() };
        assert_eq!(String::from_utf8(encode_response(&r, false, true)).unwrap(), "HTTP/1.1 201 Created\r\ncontent-type: text/plain\r\ncontent-length: 2\r\nconnection: keep-alive\r\n\r\nhi");
        assert!(String::from_utf8(encode_response(&r, true, false)).unwrap().ends_with("connection: close\r\n\r\n"), "HEAD: no body");
        let empty = Response { status: 204, headers: vec![], body: String::new() };
        assert_eq!(String::from_utf8(encode_response(&empty, false, true)).unwrap(), "HTTP/1.1 204 No Content\r\nconnection: keep-alive\r\n\r\n");
    }
}
```

Create `qal/crates/qal-serve/src/lib.rs`:

```rust
//! QAL services (spec Phase 3 §2): a stored handler `fn(HttpRequest) -> HttpResponse` served over
//! HTTP/1.1. [`service`] is the request pipeline (also used by `qal_request` without TCP),
//! [`http`] reads and writes HTTP on a connection, [`server`] accepts connections and runs the
//! workers.

pub mod http;
pub mod server;
pub mod service;

pub use http::HttpLimits;
pub use server::{bind, serve, Log, ServerConfig};
pub use service::{Handled, LogEntry, Request, Response, Service, ServiceConfig, Worker};

use std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool;
use std::sync::Arc;

/// Sets `flag` on SIGINT and SIGTERM (the server then shuts down gracefully).
pub fn on_shutdown_signals(flag: &Arc<AtomicBool>) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        signal_hook::flag::register(signal_hook::consts::SIGINT, flag.clone())?;
        signal_hook::flag::register(signal_hook::consts::SIGTERM, flag.clone())?;
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    let _ = flag;
    Ok(())
}
```

Create `qal/crates/qal-serve/src/server.rs`:

```rust
//! The synchronous HTTP server (spec Phase 3 §2.3, §2.6): one accept thread, a bounded queue of
//! connections (full: `503` with `Retry-After: 1`), and worker threads with guest-sized stacks that
//! each serve one connection at a time (keep-alive, requests in order). JSON log lines go to the
//! log sink; a panic while handling a request is answered with 500 and the worker goes on;
//! shutdown stops accepting and lets running requests finish for up to 10 s.

use crate::http::{Conn, HttpLimits, Next, Rejection};
use crate::service::{Response, Service};
use qal_run::{RunError, GUEST_THREAD_STACK};
use serde::Serialize;
use serde_json::json;
use std::io::Write;
use std::net::{SocketAddr, TcpListener, TcpStream};
use std::panic::{catch_unwind, AssertUnwindSafe};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, SyncSender, TrySendError};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// How long running requests may take to finish after a shutdown signal.
pub const SHUTDOWN_GRACE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct ServerConfig {
    pub workers: usize,
    /// Accepted connections waiting for a worker.
    pub queue: usize,
    pub http: HttpLimits,
}

impl Default for ServerConfig {
    fn default() -> ServerConfig {
        ServerConfig { workers: 4, queue: 64, http: HttpLimits::default() }
    }
}

/// Where the JSON log lines go (stderr or a file); one line per event.
pub struct Log {
    out: Mutex<Box<dyn Write + Send>>,
}

impl Log {
    pub fn new(out: Box<dyn Write + Send>) -> Log {
        Log { out: Mutex::new(out) }
    }

    pub fn stderr() -> Log {
        Log::new(Box::new(std::io::stderr()))
    }

    /// Writes one event; a failing log never stops the server.
    pub fn line(&self, event: &impl Serialize) {
        if let (Ok(text), Ok(mut out)) = (serde_json::to_string(event), self.out.lock()) {
            let _ = writeln!(out, "{text}").and_then(|_| out.flush());
        }
    }
}

/// Binds the listening address (`listen_failed`, E0802).
pub fn bind(addr: &str) -> Result<TcpListener, RunError> {
    TcpListener::bind(addr).map_err(|e| RunError::new("listen_failed", "", format!("cannot listen on {addr}: {e}")).hint("choose another address with --listen, e.g. 127.0.0.1:0 for a free port"))
}

/// A host-made log entry (rejections, overload, internal errors).
fn host_event(id: u64, req: Option<(&str, &str)>, status: u16, error: RunError) -> serde_json::Value {
    let (method, path) = req.unwrap_or(("", ""));
    json!({"event": "request", "id": id, "method": method, "path": path, "status": status, "ms": 0, "fuel": 0, "mem_bytes": 0, "db": "none", "error": error})
}

struct Shared {
    service: Arc<Service>,
    cfg: ServerConfig,
    log: Arc<Log>,
    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    served: AtomicU64,
}

/// Serves connections from `listener` until `shutdown` is set; returns the number of responses
/// sent. The caller has run the service's init function already.
pub fn serve(listener: TcpListener, service: Arc<Service>, cfg: ServerConfig, log: Arc<Log>, shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>, workers: Vec<crate::service::Worker>) -> u64 {
    let addr = listener.local_addr().ok();
    let shared = Arc::new(Shared { service, cfg, log, shutdown, served: AtomicU64::new(0) });
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::sync_channel::<TcpStream>(shared.cfg.queue.max(1));
    let rx = Arc::new(Mutex::new(rx));
    let (done_tx, done_rx) = mpsc::channel::<()>();
    let mut started = 0;
    for (k, worker) in workers.into_iter().enumerate() {
        let (shared, rx, done) = (shared.clone(), rx.clone(), done_tx.clone());
        let spawned = std::thread::Builder::new().name(format!("qal-worker-{k}")).stack_size(GUEST_THREAD_STACK).spawn(move || {
            work(&shared, &rx, worker);
            let _ = done.send(());
        });
        if spawned.is_ok() {
            started += 1;
        }
    }
    drop(done_tx);
    // Wakes the blocking `accept` once a shutdown is requested.
    let waker = {
        let shared = shared.clone();
        std::thread::Builder::new().name("qal-shutdown".into()).spawn(move || {
            while !shared.shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25));
            }
            if let Some(a) = addr {
                let _ = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&local(a), Duration::from_millis(500));
            }
        })
    };
    accept(&listener, &shared, &tx);
    drop(tx);
    drop(listener);
    let deadline = Instant::now() + SHUTDOWN_GRACE;
    for _ in 0..started {
        if done_rx.recv_timeout(deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now())).is_err() {
            break;
        }
    }
    if let Ok(w) = waker {
        let _ = w.join();
    }
    shared.served.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
}

/// An address to connect to for `a` (an unspecified listen address becomes loopback).
fn local(a: SocketAddr) -> SocketAddr {
    match a {
        SocketAddr::V4(v) if v.ip().is_unspecified() => SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], v.port())),
        SocketAddr::V6(v) if v.ip().is_unspecified() => SocketAddr::from((std::net::Ipv6Addr::LOCALHOST, v.port())),
        other => other,
    }
}

fn accept(listener: &TcpListener, shared: &Arc<Shared>, tx: &SyncSender<TcpStream>) {
    for conn in listener.incoming() {
        if shared.shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            return;
        }
        let Ok(stream) = conn else { continue };
        match tx.try_send(stream) {
            Ok(()) => {}
            Err(TrySendError::Full(stream)) => overloaded(shared, stream),
            Err(TrySendError::Disconnected(_)) => return,
        }
    }
}

/// Answers a connection the queue has no room for.
fn overloaded(shared: &Shared, stream: TcpStream) {
    let id = shared.service.next_id();
    let mut resp = Response::json(503, json!({"error": {"kind": "overloaded"}}).to_string(), id);
    resp.headers.push(("retry-after".into(), "1".into()));
    let _ = stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(1)));
    let mut conn = Conn::new(stream);
    let _ = conn.send(&resp, false, false);
    shared.served.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
    shared.log.line(&host_event(id, None, 503, RunError::new("overloaded", "", "the connection queue is full")));
}

fn work(shared: &Shared, rx: &Mutex<Receiver<TcpStream>>, mut worker: crate::service::Worker) {
    loop {
        let next = match rx.lock() {
            Ok(r) => r.recv(),
            Err(_) => return,
        };
        let Ok(stream) = next else { return };
        // The header deadline starts when a worker takes the connection, so time spent waiting
        // in the queue does not count against a client that sent its request promptly.
        let taken = Instant::now();
        // Requests are guarded inside `connection`; this catches a bug anywhere else in it.
        if catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| connection(shared, &mut worker, stream, taken))).is_err() {
            worker = crate::service::Worker::default();
        }
    }
}

/// Serves the requests of one connection in order.
fn connection(shared: &Shared, worker: &mut crate::service::Worker, stream: TcpStream, taken: Instant) {
    let limits = &shared.cfg.http;
    let mut conn = Conn::new(stream);
    loop {
        let incoming = match conn.next(limits, taken) {
            Next::Request(i) => i,
            Next::Closed => return,
            Next::Reject(r) => return reject(shared, &mut conn, r),
        };
        let req = &incoming.request;
        let id = shared.service.next_id();
        let head_only = req.method == "HEAD";
        let mut handler_req = req.clone();
        if head_only {
            handler_req.method = "GET".into();
        }
        let handled = catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| shared.service.handle(worker, &handler_req, id, incoming.head_bytes)));
        let (resp, log) = match handled {
            Ok(h) => (h.response, json!(h.log)),
            Err(p) => {
                // A bug in the host: answer, log, and continue with a fresh worker state.
                *worker = crate::service::Worker::default();
                let msg = p.downcast_ref::<&str>().map(|s| s.to_string()).or_else(|| p.downcast_ref::<String>().cloned()).unwrap_or_else(|| "panic".into());
                let body = json!({"error": {"kind": "internal_error", "id": id.to_string()}}).to_string();
                (Response::json(500, body, id), host_event(id, Some((&req.method, &req.path)), 500, RunError::new("internal", "", format!("the server panicked while handling the request: {msg}"))))
            }
        };
        let last = conn.answered + 1 >= limits.keepalive_requests;
        let keep = incoming.keep_alive && !last && !shared.shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
        let sent = conn.send(&resp, head_only, keep);
        shared.served.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        shared.log.line(&log);
        if sent.is_err() || !keep {
            return;
        }
    }
}

fn reject(shared: &Shared, conn: &mut Conn, r: Rejection) {
    let id = shared.service.next_id();
    let _ = conn.send(&r.response(id), false, false);
    shared.served.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
    shared.log.line(&host_event(id, None, r.status, RunError::new("request_rejected", "", format!("{} {}: {}", r.status, r.kind, r.message))));
}
```

Create `qal/crates/qal-serve/src/service.rs`:

```rust
//! The service pipeline (spec Phase 3 §2): one request in, one response and one log entry out.
//!
//! [`Service::handle`] is everything between the HTTP parser and the HTTP writer: the request
//! becomes an `HttpRequest` value, the handler runs in a fresh instance of the prepared module
//! (inside one database transaction when it uses `db`), and its `HttpResponse` is validated and
//! completed (`content-length`, a default `content-type`, `x-request-id`). Traps and invalid
//! responses become 500/503 responses; nothing a handler does ends the process. `qal serve` and
//! `qal_request` both run exactly this pipeline.

use qal_core::ast::Type;
use qal_core::tast::TProgram;
use qal_run::{Db, ExecIn, Limits, Permissions, RunError, Runner, Value};
use qal_wasm::Entry;
use serde::Serialize;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Limits and settings of a service (spec §2.5).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct ServiceConfig {
    /// Handler function, `fn(HttpRequest) -> HttpResponse` with any effects.
    pub entry: String,
    /// Optional init function, `fn() -> Result[Unit, Str]`, run once before serving.
    pub init: Option<String>,
    pub request_timeout: Duration,
    pub fuel: u64,
    /// Guest memory per request in bytes (also the host budget).
    pub memory: usize,
    /// Largest response body in bytes.
    pub max_response: usize,
    /// Printed output kept per request (the rest is counted).
    pub max_out: usize,
    pub budgets: Limits,
    /// Return the full runtime error in 500/503 bodies.
    pub debug_errors: bool,
    /// Instance slots of the pooling allocator (workers + 2).
    pub slots: usize,
}

impl Default for ServiceConfig {
    fn default() -> ServiceConfig {
        ServiceConfig {
            entry: "handle".into(),
            init: None,
            request_timeout: Duration::from_secs(5),
            fuel: 500_000_000,
            memory: 64 << 20,
            max_response: 8 << 20,
            max_out: 4 << 10,
            budgets: Limits::default(),
            debug_errors: false,
            slots: 6,
        }
    }
}

/// An HTTP request as the handler sees it (`HttpRequest`).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct Request {
    pub method: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub query: String,
    pub headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
    pub body: String,
}

/// The response to send: status, complete headers (including `content-length` unless the status
/// has no body) and body.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct Response {
    pub status: u16,
    pub headers: Vec<(String, String)>,
    pub body: String,
}

impl Response {
    /// A response made by the host (errors, rejections) with a JSON body.
    pub fn json(status: u16, body: String, id: u64) -> Response {
        let headers = vec![("content-type".into(), "application/json".into()), ("content-length".into(), body.len().to_string()), ("x-request-id".into(), id.to_string())];
        Response { status, headers, body }
    }

    pub fn header(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&str> {
        self.headers.iter().find(|(n, _)| n.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name)).map(|(_, v)| v.as_str())
    }
}

/// One line of the service log for a request (spec §2.6).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct LogEntry {
    pub event: &'static str,
    pub id: u64,
    pub method: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub status: u16,
    pub ms: u64,
    pub fuel: u64,
    pub mem_bytes: usize,
    pub db: &'static str,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub out: Option<String>,
    /// Bytes printed in total (more than `out` holds if it was cut).
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub out_bytes: Option<usize>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub error: Option<RunError>,
}

/// What one request produced.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Handled {
    pub response: Response,
    pub log: LogEntry,
}

/// Why a service cannot start.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct StartError {
    pub error: RunError,
}

/// A worker's resources: its own database connection (opened lazily, reopened after a failure).
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Worker {
    db: Option<Db>,
}

/// Effects a handler or init function may have.
const SERVICE_EFFECTS: [&str; 6] = ["io", "time", "rand", "db", "net", "fs"];

fn sig_text(params: &[Type], ret: &Type, effects: &[String]) -> String {
    let ps: Vec<String> = params.iter().map(|t| t.to_string()).collect();
    let effs: String = effects.iter().map(|e| format!(" !{e}")).collect();
    format!("fn({}) -> {ret}{effs}", ps.join(", "))
}

/// Checks that `name` is a function of the right shape before anything starts (`bad_entry`).
fn check_entry(typed: &TProgram, name: &str, params: &[Type], ret: &Type, what: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, RunError> {
    let expected = sig_text(params, ret, &[]);
    let Some(f) = typed.fns.iter().find(|f| f.def.name == name) else {
        let mut e = RunError::new("bad_entry", name, format!("there is no function `{name}` to use as the {what}"));
        e.hints.push(format!("the {what} must be `{expected}` (any effects)"));
        return Err(e);
    };
    let found: Vec<Type> = f.def.params.iter().map(|p| p.ty.clone()).collect();
    if found != params || f.def.ret != *ret {
        let mut e = RunError::new("bad_entry", name, format!("the {what} `{name}` must be `{expected}` (any effects), found `{}`", sig_text(&found, &f.def.ret, &f.def.effects)));
        e.hints.push(format!("define `fn {name}(...)` with exactly that signature, or choose another {what}"));
        return Err(e);
    }
    if let Some(bad) = f.def.effects.iter().find(|e| !SERVICE_EFFECTS.contains(&e.as_str())) {
        return Err(RunError::new("bad_entry", name, format!("the {what} `{name}` has the effect `{bad}`, which services do not support")));
    }
    Ok(f.def.effects.clone())
}

fn named(n: &str) -> Type {
    Type::Named(n.to_string())
}

fn headers_value(hs: &[(String, String)]) -> Value {
    Value::List(hs.iter().map(|(n, v)| Value::Record("HttpHeader".into(), vec![("name".into(), Value::Str(n.clone())), ("value".into(), Value::Str(v.clone()))])).collect())
}

/// The `HttpRequest` value of a request.
pub fn request_value(r: &Request) -> Value {
    Value::Record(
        "HttpRequest".into(),
        vec![
            ("method".into(), Value::Str(r.method.clone())),
            ("path".into(), Value::Str(r.path.clone())),
            ("query".into(), Value::Str(r.query.clone())),
            ("headers".into(), headers_value(&r.headers)),
            ("body".into(), Value::Str(r.body.clone())),
        ],
    )
}

/// Statuses whose responses carry no body (and no `content-length`).
pub fn bodiless(status: u16) -> bool {
    (100..200).contains(&status) || status == 204 || status == 304
}

fn is_token(s: &str) -> bool {
    !s.is_empty() && s.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~".contains(&b))
}

/// A handler's `HttpResponse` checked against the rules of §2.6, or why it is invalid.
fn validate(v: &Value, max_body: usize) -> Result<Response, String> {
    let Value::Record(_, fields) = v else { return Err("the handler did not return an HttpResponse".into()) };
    let (Some(Value::Int(status)), Some(Value::List(hs)), Some(Value::Str(body))) = (field(fields, "status"), field(fields, "headers"), field(fields, "body")) else {
        return Err("malformed HttpResponse".into());
    };
    let status = u16::try_from(*status).ok().filter(|s| (100..=599).contains(s)).ok_or_else(|| format!("status {status} is not in 100..599"))?;
    if body.len() > max_body {
        return Err(format!("the body has {} bytes, more than the limit of {max_body}", body.len()));
    }
    if bodiless(status) && !body.is_empty() {
        return Err(format!("a {status} response cannot have a body"));
    }
    let mut headers = Vec::new();
    for h in hs {
        let Value::Record(_, f) = h else { return Err("malformed header".into()) };
        let (Some(Value::Str(n)), Some(Value::Str(val))) = (field(f, "name"), field(f, "value")) else { return Err("malformed header".into()) };
        if !is_token(n) {
            return Err(format!("`{}` is not a valid header name", n.escape_debug()));
        }
        if val.bytes().any(|b| b == b'\r' || b == b'\n' || b == 0) {
            return Err(format!("the value of header `{n}` contains CR, LF or NUL"));
        }
        let lower = n.to_ascii_lowercase();
        if matches!(lower.as_str(), "content-length" | "transfer-encoding" | "connection") {
            return Err(format!("the handler may not set `{lower}` (the host does)"));
        }
        // The request id is always the host's.
        if lower != "x-request-id" {
            headers.push((lower, val.clone()));
        }
    }
    Ok(Response { status, headers, body: body.clone() })
}

fn field<'v>(fields: &'v [(String, Value)], name: &str) -> Option<&'v Value> {
    fields.iter().find(|(n, _)| n == name).map(|(_, v)| v)
}

pub struct Service {
    runner: Runner,
    cfg: ServiceConfig,
    next_id: AtomicU64,
    uses_db: bool,
}

impl Service {
    /// Checks the handler (and init function) and the grants, and prepares the module. Fails with
    /// `bad_entry`, `permission_denied` or `compile_error` before anything runs.
    pub fn new(typed: &TProgram, perms: Permissions, cfg: ServiceConfig) -> Result<Service, RunError> {
        let mut effects = check_entry(typed, &cfg.entry, &[named("HttpRequest")], &named("HttpResponse"), "handler")?;
        let mut entries = vec![cfg.entry.clone()];
        if let Some(init) = &cfg.init {
            effects.extend(check_entry(typed, init, &[], &Type::result(Type::Unit, Type::Str), "init function")?);
            entries.push(init.clone());
        }
        effects.sort();
        effects.dedup();
        let missing: Vec<&String> = effects.iter().filter(|e| !perms.allows(e)).collect();
        if !missing.is_empty() {
            let list: Vec<&str> = missing.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();
            let flag = list.iter().map(|e| match *e {
                "fs" => "fs:<dir>",
                "net" => "net:<host>",
                other => other,
            });
            return Err(RunError::new("permission_denied", &cfg.entry, format!("the service needs effect(s) {} which were not granted", list.join(", "))).hint(format!("grant them with `--allow {}`", flag.collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","))));
        }
        let compile_error = |m: String| RunError::new("compile_error", &cfg.entry, format!("the program cannot be compiled to WebAssembly: {m}"));
        let module = qal_wasm::compile(typed, &Entry::Fns(entries)).map_err(compile_error)?;
        let runner = Runner::prepared(module, perms, cfg.memory, cfg.slots)
            .map_err(compile_error)?
            .with_fuel(cfg.fuel)
            .with_timeout(cfg.request_timeout)
            .with_limits(cfg.budgets)
            .with_stdout_limit(cfg.max_out)
            .with_exact_results(cfg.max_response.saturating_add(1 << 20));
        let uses_db = effects.iter().any(|e| e == "db");
        Ok(Service { runner, cfg, next_id: AtomicU64::new(1), uses_db })
    }

    pub fn config(&self) -> &ServiceConfig {
        &self.cfg
    }

    /// True if executions use the pooling allocator.
    pub fn pooled(&self) -> bool {
        self.runner.pooled()
    }

    /// The prepared runner (for measurements).
    pub fn runner(&self) -> &Runner {
        &self.runner
    }

    /// A worker with its own database connection (opened now if the service uses `db`, so a
    /// broken database stops the start).
    pub fn worker(&self) -> Result<Worker, RunError> {
        let mut w = Worker::default();
        self.ensure_db(&mut w)?;
        Ok(w)
    }

    fn ensure_db(&self, w: &mut Worker) -> Result<(), RunError> {
        if !self.uses_db || w.db.is_some() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let perms = self.runner.permissions();
        if let Some(grant) = &perms.db {
            let db = Db::open(grant, &perms.db_dir(), self.cfg.budgets.db_max_mib).map_err(|m| RunError::new("db_open", &self.cfg.entry, m))?;
            w.db = Some(db);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// The next request id (monotonic per process).
    pub fn next_id(&self) -> u64 {
        self.next_id.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
    }

    /// Runs the init function (if any) in its own instance and transaction: committed only on
    /// `Ok(())`. The error is a runtime error, or `Err(message)` as `init_failed`.
    pub fn init(&self, w: &mut Worker) -> Result<(), RunError> {
        let Some(init) = &self.cfg.init else { return Ok(()) };
        self.ensure_db(w)?;
        let ok = |r: &Result<Value, RunError>| matches!(r, Ok(Value::Variant(n, _)) if n == "Ok");
        let out = self.runner.execute(init, &[], ExecIn { db: w.db.take(), host_used: 0 }, &ok);
        w.db = out.db;
        match out.outcome.result {
            Ok(Value::Variant(n, _)) if n == "Ok" => Ok(()),
            Ok(Value::Variant(_, payload)) => {
                let m = match payload.first() {
                    Some(Value::Str(m)) => m.clone(),
                    _ => String::new(),
                };
                Err(RunError::new("init_failed", init, format!("`{init}` returned Err: {m}")))
            }
            Ok(other) => Err(RunError::new("trap", init, format!("`{init}` returned {other:?}"))),
            Err(e) => Err(e),
        }
    }

    /// Handles one request on worker `w` (spec §2.2, §2.6). `head_bytes` (the request line and
    /// headers as received) count against the host budget together with the body.
    pub fn handle(&self, w: &mut Worker, req: &Request, id: u64, head_bytes: usize) -> Handled {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let mut log = LogEntry { event: "request", id, method: req.method.clone(), path: req.path.clone(), status: 500, ms: 0, fuel: 0, mem_bytes: 0, db: "none", out: None, out_bytes: None, error: None };
        if let Err(e) = self.ensure_db(w) {
            return self.failed(log, e, start);
        }
        let max = self.cfg.max_response;
        let commit = |r: &Result<Value, RunError>| matches!(r, Ok(v) if validate(v, max).is_ok_and(|resp| resp.status < 400));
        let input = ExecIn { db: w.db.take(), host_used: head_bytes.saturating_add(req.body.len()) };
        let out = self.runner.execute(&self.cfg.entry, &[request_value(req)], input, &commit);
        w.db = out.db;
        log.fuel = out.fuel;
        log.mem_bytes = out.mem_bytes;
        log.db = out.outcome.db;
        if out.outcome.stdout_bytes > 0 {
            log.out = Some(out.outcome.stdout.clone());
            log.out_bytes = Some(out.outcome.stdout_bytes);
        }
        let result = out.outcome.result.and_then(|v| validate(&v, max).map_err(|m| RunError::new("bad_response", &self.cfg.entry, format!("invalid response: {m}"))));
        match result {
            Ok(mut resp) => {
                if resp.header("content-type").is_none() {
                    resp.headers.push(("content-type".into(), "text/plain; charset=utf-8".into()));
                }
                if !bodiless(resp.status) {
                    resp.headers.push(("content-length".into(), resp.body.len().to_string()));
                }
                resp.headers.push(("x-request-id".into(), id.to_string()));
                log.status = resp.status;
                log.ms = start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
                Handled { response: resp, log }
            }
            Err(e) => self.failed(log, e, start),
        }
    }

    /// The 500/503 response for a runtime error (`timeout` is 503).
    fn failed(&self, mut log: LogEntry, e: RunError, start: Instant) -> Handled {
        let (status, kind) = if e.kind == "timeout" { (503, "timeout") } else { (500, "internal_error") };
        let body = if self.cfg.debug_errors {
            serde_json::json!({"error": e, "id": log.id.to_string()}).to_string()
        } else {
            serde_json::json!({"error": {"kind": kind, "id": log.id.to_string()}}).to_string()
        };
        log.status = status;
        log.ms = start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;
        log.error = Some(e);
        Handled { response: Response::json(status, body, log.id), log }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn rec(status: i64, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> Value {
        Value::Record(
            "HttpResponse".into(),
            vec![
                ("status".into(), Value::Int(status)),
                ("headers".into(), headers_value(&headers.iter().map(|(n, v)| (n.to_string(), v.to_string())).collect::<Vec<_>>())),
                ("body".into(), Value::Str(body.into())),
            ],
        )
    }

    #[test]
    fn responses_are_validated() {
        assert_eq!(validate(&rec(200, &[("X-A", "1")], "hi"), 10).unwrap(), Response { status: 200, headers: vec![("x-a".into(), "1".into())], body: "hi".into() });
        let e = |v: Value| validate(&v, 10).unwrap_err();
        assert!(e(rec(99, &[], "")).contains("not in 100..599"));
        assert!(e(rec(600, &[], "")).contains("not in 100..599"));
        assert!(e(rec(200, &[], "01234567890")).contains("more than the limit"));
        assert!(e(rec(204, &[], "x")).contains("cannot have a body"));
        assert!(e(rec(200, &[("x", "a\r\nset-cookie: y")], "")).contains("CR, LF or NUL"));
        assert!(e(rec(200, &[("bad name", "v")], "")).contains("not a valid header name"));
        assert!(e(rec(200, &[("", "v")], "")).contains("not a valid header name"));
        for h in ["Content-Length", "transfer-encoding", "Connection"] {
            assert!(e(rec(200, &[(h, "1")], "")).contains("may not set"), "{h}");
        }
        assert!(validate(&rec(200, &[("x-request-id", "mine")], ""), 10).unwrap().headers.is_empty(), "the host sets the request id");
        assert!(bodiless(204) && bodiless(304) && bodiless(101) && !bodiless(200));
    }
}
```

```rust
/// A project with the fixture service of qal-serve stored in it.
fn service_project(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let project = temp_dir().join(name);
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&project);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&project).unwrap();
    let app = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../qal-serve/tests/fixtures/app.qal");
    assert_eq!(qal_json_in(&project, &["init"]).0, 0);
    let (code, v) = qal_json_in(&project, &["define", app.to_str().unwrap()]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{v}");
    project
}

#[test]
fn qal_request_runs_a_json_file_of_requests() {
    let project = service_project("request");
    std::fs::write(project.join("r.json"), r#"{"requests": [{"method": "POST", "path": "/todos", "body": "a"}, {"path": "/todos"}]}"#).unwrap();
    let (code, v) = qal_json_in(&project, &["request", "--allow", "db,io", "--init", "setup", "r.json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{v}");
    assert_eq!(v["responses"][1]["json"], json!([{"id": 1, "title": "a"}]));
    assert!(project.join(".qal/db/main.sqlite").is_file());
    let (code, v) = qal_json_in(&project, &["request", "handle", "--allow", "db,io", "r.json"]);
    assert_eq!((code, v["responses"][1]["json"].as_array().map(|a| a.len())), (0, Some(2)), "the project's database persists");
    std::fs::write(project.join("bad.json"), r#"[{"path": 5}]"#).unwrap();
    let out = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_qal")).current_dir(&project).args(["request", "--allow", "db", "bad.json"]).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(2));
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("request 0: request field `path` must be a string"));
}

#[test]
fn qal_serve_answers_http_and_stops_on_sigterm() {
    use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Read, Write};
    let project = service_project("serve");
    let mut child = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_qal"))
        .current_dir(&project)
        .args(["serve", "--listen", "127.0.0.1:0", "--allow", "db,io", "--init", "setup", "--workers", "2", "--request-timeout", "800"])
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(Stdio::null())
        .stderr(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    let mut log = BufReader::new(child.stderr.take().unwrap());
    let mut line = String::new();
    log.read_line(&mut line).unwrap();
    let listening: Value = serde_json::from_str(&line).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}: {line}"));
    assert_eq!((listening["event"].as_str(), listening["entry"].as_str(), listening["workers"].as_u64()), (Some("listening"), Some("handle"), Some(2)));
    assert_eq!(listening["hash"].as_str().map(str::len), Some(64));
    let addr: std::net::SocketAddr = listening["addr"].as_str().unwrap().parse().unwrap();
    let send = |raw: &str| {
        let mut s = std::net::TcpStream::connect(addr).unwrap();
        s.write_all(raw.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        let mut out = String::new();
        s.read_to_string(&mut out).unwrap();
        out
    };
    let created = send("POST /todos HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\nContent-Length: 3\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\negg");
    assert!(created.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 201 Created\r\n") && created.ends_with(r#"{"id":1,"title":"egg"}"#), "{created}");
    let spin = send("GET /spin HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n");
    assert!(spin.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 503 "), "{spin}");
    let mut request = String::new();
    log.read_line(&mut request).unwrap();
    let request: Value = serde_json::from_str(&request).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((request["event"].as_str(), request["status"].as_u64(), request["db"].as_str()), (Some("request"), Some(201), Some("commit")));
    assert!(Command::new("kill").args(["-TERM", &child.id().to_string()]).status().unwrap().success());
    let status = child.wait().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(status.code(), Some(0));
    let rest: Vec<Value> = log.lines().map(|l| serde_json::from_str(&l.unwrap()).unwrap()).collect();
    assert_eq!(rest.last().unwrap(), &json!({"event": "stopped", "served": 2}));
    // Start failures: a bad handler is exit 1 with the error as JSON; `db:mem` is a usage error.
    let out = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_qal")).current_dir(&project).args(["serve", "text", "--listen", "127.0.0.1:0", "--allow", "db,io"]).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(1));
    let failed: Value = serde_json::from_str(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).lines().next().unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((failed["event"].as_str(), failed["error"]["code"].as_str()), (Some("start_failed"), Some("E0520")));
    let out = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_qal")).current_dir(&project).args(["serve", "--allow", "db:mem,io"]).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(2));
    let out = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_qal")).current_dir(&project).args(["serve", "--workers", "0", "--allow", "db,io"]).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(out.status.code(), Some(2));
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("`--workers` must be a number in 1..=64"));
}
```

```rust
fn fixture_app() -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../qal-serve/tests/fixtures/app.qal")).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn agents_test_services_with_qal_request() {
    let mut c = Client::start();
    c.initialize();
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_define", json!({"source": fixture_app()}));
    assert!(!err, "{v}");
    let requests = json!([
        {"method": "POST", "path": "/todos", "body": "milk"},
        {"method": "POST", "path": "/fail"},
        {"path": "/todos", "json": null},
        {"path": "/todos/1"},
        {"method": "HEAD", "path": "/todos"},
        {"path": "/trap"},
        {"path": "/print"},
        {"method": "PUT", "path": "/echo", "query": "a=1", "headers": [{"name": "X-Test", "value": " v "}, {"name": "Connection", "value": "close"}], "json": {"k": [1, 2]}},
    ]);
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"init": "setup", "allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": requests}));
    assert!(!err, "{v}");
    let r = v["responses"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(r.len(), 8);
    assert_eq!((r[0]["status"].as_u64(), r[0]["db"].as_str(), r[0]["json"].clone()), (Some(201), Some("commit"), json!({"id": 1, "title": "milk"})));
    assert!(r[0]["headers"].as_array().unwrap().contains(&json!({"name": "location", "value": "/todos/1"})), "{}", r[0]);
    assert_eq!((r[1]["status"].as_u64(), r[1]["db"].as_str()), (Some(422), Some("rollback")));
    assert_eq!(r[2]["json"], json!([{"id": 1, "title": "milk"}]), "the 422 rolled its insert back; requests share the database");
    assert_eq!(r[3]["body"], r#"{"id":1,"title":"milk"}"#);
    assert_eq!((r[4]["status"].as_u64(), r[4]["body"].as_str()), (Some(200), Some("")));
    assert_eq!((r[5]["status"].as_u64(), r[5]["error"]["kind"].as_str(), r[5]["db"].as_str()), (Some(500), Some("int_overflow"), Some("rollback")));
    assert_eq!(r[6]["stdout"], "hello from the handler\n");
    assert_eq!(r[7]["body"], "PUT /echo?a=1 x-test,content-type,content-length {\"k\":[1,2]}", "lowercase names, no hop-by-hop headers");
    // A fresh in-memory database per call; the project's file only with "db".
    let (_, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"init": "setup", "allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": [{"path": "/todos"}]}));
    assert_eq!(v["responses"][0]["json"], json!([]), "{v}");
    assert!(!c.root.join(".qal/db").exists());
    // Errors before anything runs.
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"requests": [{"path": "/health"}]}));
    assert_eq!((err, v["error"]["kind"].as_str()), (true, Some("permission_denied")));
    assert_eq!(v["error"]["hints"], json!(["pass `\"allow\": [\"db:mem\", \"io\"]` to qal_run, qal_call or qal_request"]));
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"handler": "text", "allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": []}));
    assert_eq!((err, v["error"]["code"].as_str()), (true, Some("E0520")));
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"init": "nope", "allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": []}));
    assert_eq!((err, v["error"]["kind"].as_str()), (true, Some("bad_entry")));
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": [{"path": "todos"}]}));
    assert!(err && v["diagnostics"][0]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("must start with `/`"), "{v}");
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": [{"path": "/", "verb": "GET"}]}));
    assert!(err && v["diagnostics"][0]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("unknown request field `verb`"), "{v}");
    let many: Vec<Value> = (0..51).map(|_| json!({"path": "/health"})).collect();
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": many}));
    assert!(err && v["diagnostics"][0]["message"].as_str().unwrap().contains("at most 50"), "{v}");
    let (err, v) = c.call("qal_request", json!({"allow": ["db:mem", "io"], "requests": [{"method": "POST", "path": "/todos", "body": "x".repeat((1 << 20) + 1)}]}));
    assert_eq!((err, v["responses"][0]["status"].as_u64(), v["responses"][0]["error"]["code"].as_str()), (false, Some(413), Some("E0800")));
    let reference = qal_cli::mcp::REFERENCE;
    for needle in ["fn handle(rq: HttpRequest) -> HttpResponse", "string literal with `?` placeholders", "committed only for status 100-399", "\"db:mem\"", "qal_query name \"std.\""] {
        assert!(reference.contains(needle), "{needle}");
    }
    assert_eq!(c.finish(), Some(0));
}
```

```rust
//!   qal run <file> [--allow <spec>] [--] [args..]    -> {"exit_code":n,"stdout":".."} | {"error":{...},"stdout":".."} | {"diagnostics":[...]}
//!   qal run <name> [--allow <spec>] [--] [args..]    (store; `<name>` is any `fn(List[Str]) -> Int`)
//! For agents:
```

```rust
/// The options of `qal serve` with their defaults (spec Phase 3 §7.1).
const SERVE_OPTIONS: [&str; 18] = [
    "listen", "allow", "init", "workers", "queue", "request-timeout", "fuel", "memory", "max-body", "max-header-bytes", "max-response",
    "header-timeout", "body-timeout", "net-calls", "fs-write-budget", "db-max-rows", "db-max-mib", "log",
];

const SERVE_USAGE: &str = "usage: qal serve [<handler>] [--listen 127.0.0.1:8080] [--allow <spec>] [--init <fn>] [--workers 4 (1-64)] [--queue 64]\n       \
                           [--request-timeout 5000 (ms)] [--fuel 500000000] [--memory 64 (MiB, at most 256)] [--max-body 1024 (KiB)]\n       \
                           [--max-header-bytes 16384] [--max-response 8192 (KiB)] [--header-timeout 5000 (ms)] [--body-timeout 10000 (ms)]\n       \
                           [--net-calls 16] [--fs-write-budget 64 (MiB)] [--db-max-rows 10000] [--db-max-mib 1024] [--debug-errors] [--log <file>]";

/// A number option within `range` (default `default`).
fn number(s: &Split, name: &str, default: u64, range: std::ops::RangeInclusive<u64>) -> Result<u64, String> {
    match s.one(name) {
        None => Ok(default),
        Some(v) => v.parse::<u64>().ok().filter(|n| range.contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| format!("`--{name}` must be a number in {}..={}, got `{v}`", range.start(), range.end())),
    }
}

/// Writes a start failure (stderr and the log) and exits 1.
fn start_failed(log: &qal_serve::Log, error: &qal_run::RunError) -> ExitCode {
    log.line(&serde_json::json!({"event": "start_failed", "error": error}));
    ExitCode::from(1)
}

fn serve(args: &[String]) -> ExitCode {
    // `--debug-errors` is the only option without a value.
    let debug_errors = args.iter().any(|a| a == "--debug-errors");
    if args.iter().any(|a| a == "--help" || a == "-h") {
        return bad_usage(SERVE_USAGE);
    }
    let args: Vec<String> = args.iter().filter(|a| *a != "--debug-errors").cloned().collect();
    let s = match split(&args, &SERVE_OPTIONS, &["allow"], &all) {
        Ok(s) if s.targets.len() <= 1 && s.rest.is_empty() => s,
        Ok(_) => return bad_usage(SERVE_USAGE),
        Err(code) => return code,
    };
    let handler = s.targets.first().map_or("handle", String::as_str);
    let parsed = (|| -> Result<(qal_serve::ServiceConfig, qal_serve::ServerConfig), String> {
        let ms = |n: &str, d: u64| number(&s, n, d, 1..=3_600_000).map(std::time::Duration::from_millis);
        let workers = number(&s, "workers", 4, 1..=64)? as usize;
        let http = qal_serve::HttpLimits {
            max_header_bytes: number(&s, "max-header-bytes", 16 << 10, 1024..=1 << 20)? as usize,
            max_body: number(&s, "max-body", 1024, 0..=64 << 10)? as usize * 1024,
            header_timeout: ms("header-timeout", 5000)?,
            body_timeout: ms("body-timeout", 10_000)?,
            ..qal_serve::HttpLimits::default()
        };
        let budgets = qal_run::Limits {
            net_calls: number(&s, "net-calls", 16, 0..=10_000)? as usize,
            fs_write_bytes: number(&s, "fs-write-budget", 64, 0..=1 << 20)? as usize * (1 << 20),
            db_max_rows: number(&s, "db-max-rows", 10_000, 1..=10_000_000)? as usize,
            db_max_mib: number(&s, "db-max-mib", 1024, 1..=1 << 20)? as usize,
            ..qal_run::Limits::default()
        };
        let svc = qal_serve::ServiceConfig {
            entry: handler.to_string(),
            init: s.one("init").map(str::to_string),
            request_timeout: ms("request-timeout", 5000)?,
            fuel: number(&s, "fuel", 500_000_000, 1..=u64::MAX / 2)?,
            memory: number(&s, "memory", 64, 1..=256)? as usize * (1 << 20),
            max_response: number(&s, "max-response", 8192, 0..=256 << 10)? as usize * 1024,
            budgets,
            debug_errors,
            slots: workers + 2,
            ..qal_serve::ServiceConfig::default()
        };
        Ok((svc, qal_serve::ServerConfig { workers, queue: number(&s, "queue", 64, 1..=100_000)? as usize, http }))
    })();
    let (svc, srv) = match parsed {
        Ok(c) => c,
        Err(e) => return bad_usage(&e),
    };
    let perms = match permissions(&s) {
        Ok(p) if p.db == Some(qal_run::DbGrant::Memory) => return bad_usage("`db:mem` is a database per tool call; a server needs a file: `db` or `db:<name>`"),
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(e) => return bad_usage(&e),
    };
    let log = Arc::new(match s.one("log") {
        Some(path) => match std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(path) {
            Ok(f) => qal_serve::Log::new(Box::new(f)),
            Err(e) => return bad_usage(&format!("cannot open the log {path}: {e}")),
        },
        None => qal_serve::Log::stderr(),
    });
    let ctx = match ctx() {
        Ok(c) => c,
        Err(code) => return code,
    };
    let mut store = match ctx.open() {
        Ok(st) => st,
        Err(d) => return emit(Reply::diagnostics(d)),
    };
    // .qal/store.db -> the project, whose .qal/db holds the database files.
    let perms = match store.path().parent().and_then(std::path::Path::parent) {
        Some(project) if perms.db.is_some() => perms.with_db_dir(project.join(qal_store::STORE_DIR).join("db")),
        _ => perms,
    };
    let loaded = match store.program().and_then(qal_cli::load_program) {
        Ok(l) => l,
        Err(d) => return emit(Reply::diagnostics(d)),
    };
    let hash = store.query(&Query { name: Some(handler.to_string()), ..Query::default() }).ok().and_then(|rs| rs.into_iter().find(|e| e.name == handler)).map(|e| e.hash).unwrap_or_default();
    let service = match qal_serve::Service::new(&loaded.typed, perms, svc) {
        Ok(sv) => Arc::new(sv),
        Err(e) => return start_failed(&log, &e),
    };
    let mut workers = Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..srv.workers {
        match service.worker() {
            Ok(w) => workers.push(w),
            Err(e) => return start_failed(&log, &e),
        }
    }
    if let Some(first) = workers.first_mut() {
        if let Err(e) = service.init(first) {
            return start_failed(&log, &e);
        }
    }
    let listen = s.one("listen").unwrap_or("127.0.0.1:8080");
    let listener = match qal_serve::bind(listen) {
        Ok(l) => l,
        Err(e) => return start_failed(&log, &e),
    };
    let shutdown = Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false));
    if let Err(e) = qal_serve::on_shutdown_signals(&shutdown) {
        return start_failed(&log, &qal_run::RunError::new("listen_failed", handler, format!("cannot install the signal handlers: {e}")));
    }
    let addr = listener.local_addr().map(|a| a.to_string()).unwrap_or_default();
    log.line(&serde_json::json!({"event": "listening", "addr": addr, "entry": handler, "hash": hash, "workers": srv.workers, "pooling": service.pooled()}));
    let served = qal_serve::serve(listener, service, srv, log.clone(), shutdown, workers);
    log.line(&serde_json::json!({"event": "stopped", "served": served}));
    ExitCode::SUCCESS
}

fn request(args: &[String]) -> ExitCode {
    let s = match split(args, &["allow", "init"], &["allow"], &all) {
        Ok(s) if (1..=2).contains(&s.targets.len()) && s.rest.is_empty() => s,
        Ok(_) => return usage(),
        Err(code) => return code,
    };
    let (handler, file) = match s.targets.as_slice() {
        [file] => ("handle", file),
        [handler, file] => (handler.as_str(), file),
        _ => return usage(),
    };
    let perms = match permissions(&s) {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(e) => return bad_usage(&e),
    };
    let text = match read(file) {
        Ok(t) => t,
        Err(code) => return code,
    };
    let specs = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text).map_err(|e| format!("{file}: not JSON: {e}")).and_then(|v| {
        let list = match v {
            serde_json::Value::Array(xs) => xs,
            serde_json::Value::Object(mut m) => match m.remove("requests") {
                Some(serde_json::Value::Array(xs)) => xs,
                _ => return Err(format!("{file}: expected an array of requests or {{\"requests\": [...]}}")),
            },
            _ => return Err(format!("{file}: expected an array of requests or {{\"requests\": [...]}}")),
        };
        list.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, r)| ops::RequestSpec::from_json(r).map_err(|e| format!("{file}: request {i}: {e}"))).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
    }) {
        Ok(specs) => specs,
        Err(e) => return bad_usage(&e),
    };
    with_ctx(|c| ops::request(c, &Source::Store, perms, handler, s.one("init"), &specs))
}
```

```rust
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
```

```rust
    /// The results of `request`, one per request, in order.

/// One request of `request` / `qal_request` (spec Phase 3 §7.2).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct RequestSpec {
    pub request: qal_serve::Request,
}

/// Most requests per `request` call.
pub const MAX_REQUESTS: usize = 50;

impl RequestSpec {
    /// Parses `{method?, path, query?, headers?, body? | json?}`. Header names are lowercased and
    /// hop-by-hop headers dropped, as the HTTP layer of `qal serve` does.
    pub fn from_json(v: &serde_json::Value) -> Result<RequestSpec, String> {
        use serde_json::Value as J;
        let J::Object(m) = v else { return Err("a request must be an object `{method, path, query, headers, body | json}`".into()) };
        if let Some(k) = m.keys().find(|k| !["method", "path", "query", "headers", "body", "json"].contains(&k.as_str())) {
            return Err(format!("unknown request field `{k}` (fields: method, path, query, headers, body, json)"));
        }
        let text = |k: &str, default: &str| -> Result<String, String> {
            match m.get(k) {
                None | Some(J::Null) => Ok(default.to_string()),
                Some(J::String(s)) => Ok(s.clone()),
                Some(_) => Err(format!("request field `{k}` must be a string")),
            }
        };
        let method = text("method", "GET")?;
        if method.is_empty() || !method.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~".contains(&b)) {
            return Err(format!("`{method}` is not an HTTP method"));
        }
        let path = text("path", "")?;
        if !path.starts_with('/') || path.contains('?') || path.contains(char::is_whitespace) {
            return Err(format!("`path` must start with `/` and contain no `?` or whitespace (the query goes in `query`), got `{path}`"));
        }
        let query = text("query", "")?;
        let mut headers = Vec::new();
        match m.get("headers") {
            None | Some(J::Null) => {}
            Some(J::Array(hs)) => {
                for h in hs {
                    let (Some(J::String(n)), Some(J::String(val))) = (h.get("name"), h.get("value")) else { return Err("each header must be `{\"name\": \"...\", \"value\": \"...\"}`".into()) };
                    if n.is_empty() || !n.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~".contains(&b)) {
                        return Err(format!("`{n}` is not a header name"));
                    }
                    if val.bytes().any(|b| b == b'\r' || b == b'\n' || b == 0) {
                        return Err(format!("the value of header `{n}` contains CR, LF or NUL"));
                    }
                    let n = n.to_ascii_lowercase();
                    if !["connection", "keep-alive", "transfer-encoding", "upgrade", "te", "trailer"].contains(&n.as_str()) {
                        headers.push((n, val.trim().to_string()));
                    }
                }
            }
            Some(_) => return Err("`headers` must be an array".into()),
        }
        // `json: null` is no JSON body (as in a GET).
        let body = match (m.get("body").filter(|b| !b.is_null()), m.get("json").filter(|j| !j.is_null())) {
            (Some(_), Some(_)) => return Err("give either `body` or `json`, not both".into()),
            (None, Some(j)) => {
                if !headers.iter().any(|(n, _)| n == "content-type") {
                    headers.push(("content-type".into(), "application/json".into()));
                }
                j.to_string()
            }
            _ => text("body", "")?,
        };
        if !headers.iter().any(|(n, _)| n == "content-length") && !body.is_empty() {
            headers.push(("content-length".into(), body.len().to_string()));
        }
        Ok(RequestSpec { request: qal_serve::Request { method, path, query, headers, body } })
    }
}

/// The result of one request (spec Phase 3 §7.2).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct RequestResult {
    pub status: u16,
    pub headers: Vec<qal_run::Value>,
    pub body: String,
    /// The body parsed, when the response is JSON.
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub json: Option<serde_json::Value>,
    /// `commit`, `rollback` or `none`.
    pub db: &'static str,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub stdout: Option<String>,
    /// The full runtime error of a failed request.
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub error: Option<RunError>,
}
```
